# Observer Stack P42 - AGOT: Royal Guards (3733779333) tooltip-mode variable reads.
#
# agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect already initialises both
# numeric variables at its top, and the author's comment says that is exactly so
# 'comparisons never dereference an unset variable'. That works at execution time.
# It does not work while CK3 is BUILDING A TOOLTIP: in that mode set_variable is
# a no-op preview, so the initialiser never lands and the comparisons below still
# read an unset variable.
#
# Called from agot_guard_school_admission.0004's option via
# agot_guard_positions_enroll_guard_trainee_effect, that produced three errors per
# tooltip evaluation, re-fired every frame the admission window was open:
#   16,893 x Invalid left side during comparison 'var'
#   16,893 x Event target link 'var' returned an unset scope
#   16,893 x Failed to fetch variable for '...training_merit' due to not being set
# 50,679 entries, 73% of the run's error log. Cosmetic - enrollment itself is
# correct - but it costs a tooltip rebuild every frame.
#
# Each read is now guarded with has_variable, which is safe in both modes.

# ============================================================================
# Observer Stack P41 - AGOT: Royal Guards (3733779333) queue flood, second pass
#
# P39 gated the .1006 fan-out, but agot_guard_positions.1007 has FIVE trigger
# sites, not one. The other four live in the departure-cleanup effects here and
# in 06_agot_swords_of_braavos_effects.txt.
#
# Those four are already guarded by agot_guard_positions_departure_cleanup_complete
# -- but that flag is REMOVED on rejoin (lines 2190 / 2198 / 2216 of the upstream
# file). Any character who churns through guard membership re-arms the whole
# cycle every time, and each pass queues another .1007 at days=10 plus a .1004
# on the liege at days=1.
#
# In the 8183 run that produced 14,116 .1007 across three characters and 12,104
# .1004 on one, 72% of the pending-event queue, and the engine began logging
#   jomini_event_queue_manager.cpp:454  Event agot_guard_positions.1007 has been
#   queued twice
# The daily tick could not drain the queue and the clock stopped.
#
# Both events are idempotent sweeps, so this cap loses nothing: a duration flag
# the rejoin path does not clear, sized just past each event's own delay.
#   .1007 -> at most one per character per 11 days (event delay 10)
#   .1004 -> at most one per liege per 2 days (event delay 1)
#
# Only the churn path is gated. The 31 interaction and decision call sites are
# player- and AI-initiated one-offs and are deliberately left alone.
# ============================================================================
agot_guard_positions_create_guard_sword_effect = {
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }

	set_artifact_rarity_masterwork = yes

	create_artifact = {
		name = agot_guard_positions_guard_sword_name
		description = $DESCRIPTION$
		visuals = longsword
		type = sword
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		modifier = kingsguard_sword_modifier
		template = agot_guard_positions_sworn_equipment_template
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		decaying = no
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					scope:owner = { agot_guard_positions_is_swords_of_braavos_trigger = yes }
					scope:owner = {
						NOT = {
						any_equipped_character_artifact = {
							artifact_slot_type = primary_armament
							OR = {
								rarity = illustrious
								rarity = famed
								rarity = masterwork
							}
						}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			equip_artifact_to_owner_replace = yes
		}
		set_variable = { name = kingsguard_sword_artifact value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_guard_sword_artifact value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = historical_unique_artifact value = yes }
	}
}

# The actual commissioned blade continues to the dead guard's family. A squire
# receives a separate memorial token, so no unique or Valyrian weapon is cloned.
agot_guard_positions_create_squire_memorial_effect = {
	set_artifact_rarity_masterwork = yes
	create_artifact = {
		name = agot_guard_positions_squire_memorial_name
		description = agot_guard_positions_squire_memorial_description
		creator = $DEAD_GUARD$
		visuals = trinket_icon
		type = miscellaneous_when_not_court
		modifier = agot_guard_positions_squire_memorial_modifier
		wealth = 20
		quality = 40
		template = agot_guard_positions_sworn_equipment_template
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		decaying = no
	}
	scope:newly_created_artifact ?= { set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_squire_memorial value = yes } }
}

# Builds the seven-position current roster used by the Guard Roster window.
# $ORDER$ is the council-position prefix; the commander is kept separate so the
# GUI can render the Kingsguard-style one-plus-six hierarchy.
agot_guard_positions_build_current_roster_for_order_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:$ORDER$_lord_commander }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_debug_commander value = cp:$ORDER$_lord_commander }
	}
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:$ORDER$_1 } add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_guard_positions_debug_current_guards target = cp:$ORDER$_1 } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:$ORDER$_2 } add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_guard_positions_debug_current_guards target = cp:$ORDER$_2 } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:$ORDER$_3 } add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_guard_positions_debug_current_guards target = cp:$ORDER$_3 } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:$ORDER$_4 } add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_guard_positions_debug_current_guards target = cp:$ORDER$_4 } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:$ORDER$_5 } add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_guard_positions_debug_current_guards target = cp:$ORDER$_5 } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:$ORDER$_6 } add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_guard_positions_debug_current_guards target = cp:$ORDER$_6 } }
}

agot_guard_positions_promote_standard_commander_effect = {
	$GUARD$ = { save_scope_as = promoted_guard }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:$COMMANDER_POSITION$ } }
		scope:promoted_guard = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_commander_promotion_in_progress add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard }
		fire_councillor = scope:promoted_guard
		scope:promoted_guard = {
			add_character_flag = $MEMBER_FLAG$
			add_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			add_trait = $MEMBER_TRAIT$
			remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		}
		assign_councillor_type = { type = $COMMANDER_POSITION$ target = scope:promoted_guard }
		scope:promoted_guard = {
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_commander_promotion_in_progress
			agot_guard_positions_record_lord_commander_effect = { HOLDER = root }
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_promote_winged_knight_commander_effect = {
	agot_guard_positions_promote_standard_commander_effect = {
		GUARD = $GUARD$
		COMMANDER_POSITION = winged_knight_lord_commander
		MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
		MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_winged_knight
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_promote_battleguard_commander_effect = {
	agot_guard_positions_promote_standard_commander_effect = {
		GUARD = $GUARD$
		COMMANDER_POSITION = battleguard_lord_commander
		MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
		MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_battleguard
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_promote_rainbow_guard_commander_effect = {
	agot_guard_positions_promote_standard_commander_effect = {
		GUARD = $GUARD$
		COMMANDER_POSITION = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
		MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
		MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_rainbowguard
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_promote_kingsmen_commander_effect = {
	agot_guard_positions_promote_standard_commander_effect = {
		GUARD = $GUARD$
		COMMANDER_POSITION = kingsmen_lord_commander
		MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member
		MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_kingsmen
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_promote_queensguard_commander_effect = {
	$GUARD$ = { save_scope_as = promoted_queensguard }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:queensguard_lord_commander } }
		if = { limit = { scope:promoted_queensguard = { has_council_position = queensguard_1 } } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_1 }
		else_if = { limit = { scope:promoted_queensguard = { has_council_position = queensguard_2 } } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_2 }
		else_if = { limit = { scope:promoted_queensguard = { has_council_position = queensguard_3 } } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_3 }
		else_if = { limit = { scope:promoted_queensguard = { has_council_position = queensguard_4 } } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_4 }
		else_if = { limit = { scope:promoted_queensguard = { has_council_position = queensguard_5 } } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_5 }
		else_if = { limit = { scope:promoted_queensguard = { has_council_position = queensguard_6 } } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_6 }
		scope:promoted_queensguard = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_commander_promotion_in_progress add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard }
		fire_councillor = scope:promoted_queensguard
		agot_guard_positions_employ_queensguard_effect = { GUARD = scope:promoted_queensguard }
		scope:promoted_queensguard = { remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_commander_promotion_in_progress }
	}
}

# A dismissed or dead custom Lord Commander must be replaced from the sworn
# ordinary roster before recruitment repairs the newly empty seventh place.
# Reuse the established commander-choice event while reserving the commander
# office from the generic repair pulse.
agot_guard_positions_prepare_commander_replacement_effect = {
	clear_variable_list = inherited_guard_keep_list
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_battleguard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_rainbow_guard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_kingsmen
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_winged_knights
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_swords_of_braavos
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_greenhand
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_queensguard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_native_kingsguard
	add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_review_pending
	add_character_flag = $ORDER_FLAG$
	every_living_character = {
		limit = {
			has_character_flag = $MEMBER_FLAG$
			agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes
		}
		root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = inherited_guard_keep_list target = prev } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_in_list = { variable = inherited_guard_keep_list always = yes } }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1012 days = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		agot_guard_positions_clear_inheritance_review_state_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_queue_vacancy_event_effect = yes
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_sword_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { this = character:Tarth_6 }
		agot_guard_positions_ensure_brienne_oathkeeper_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_character_artifact = {
				has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_sword_artifact
			}
		}
		random_character_artifact = {
			limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_sword_artifact }
			save_scope_as = agot_guard_positions_guard_sword
		}
		scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_sword ?= {
			equip_artifact_to_owner_replace = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_create_guard_sword_effect = { OWNER = this DESCRIPTION = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_sword_description }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_create_guard_sword_effect = { OWNER = this DESCRIPTION = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_sword_description }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_kingsmen_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_create_guard_sword_effect = { OWNER = this DESCRIPTION = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_sword_description }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_swords_of_braavos_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_create_guard_sword_effect = { OWNER = this DESCRIPTION = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_sword_description }
	}
	else = {
		agot_guard_positions_create_guard_sword_effect = { OWNER = this DESCRIPTION = agot_guard_positions_guard_sword_description }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_sword_artifacts_effect = {
	every_character_artifact = {
		limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_sword_artifact }
		destroy_artifact = this
	}
}


# Dismissal removes guard-issued ordinary and commissioned Valyrian-steel weapons.
agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_service_weapon_artifacts_effect = {
	every_character_artifact = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_sword_artifact
				has_variable = agot_guard_positions_valyrian_steel_guard_sword_artifact
				has_variable = agot_guard_positions_valyrian_steel_guard_weapon_artifact
			}
		}
		destroy_artifact = this
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_mark_brienne_oathkeeper_effect = {
	equip_artifact_to_owner_replace = yes
	set_variable = { name = valyrian_steel value = yes }
	set_variable = { name = oathkeeper_artifact value = yes }
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_brienne_oathkeeper_artifact value = yes }
	set_variable = { name = historical_unique_artifact value = yes }
}

agot_guard_positions_ensure_brienne_oathkeeper_effect = {
	save_scope_as = owner
	save_scope_as = agot_guard_positions_brienne_oathkeeper_owner
	agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_sword_artifacts_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_character_artifact = {
				OR = {
					has_variable = oathkeeper_artifact
					has_variable = agot_guard_positions_brienne_oathkeeper_artifact
				}
			}
		}
		random_character_artifact = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_variable = oathkeeper_artifact
					has_variable = agot_guard_positions_brienne_oathkeeper_artifact
				}
			}
			agot_guard_positions_mark_brienne_oathkeeper_effect = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_artifact = {
				OR = {
					has_variable = oathkeeper_artifact
					has_variable = agot_guard_positions_brienne_oathkeeper_artifact
				}
			}
		}
		random_artifact = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_variable = oathkeeper_artifact
					has_variable = agot_guard_positions_brienne_oathkeeper_artifact
				}
			}
			set_owner = scope:agot_guard_positions_brienne_oathkeeper_owner
			agot_guard_positions_mark_brienne_oathkeeper_effect = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_sword_artifacts_effect = yes
		create_artifact_vs_oathkeeper_historical_effect = { OWNER = this }
		scope:newly_created_artifact ?= {
			agot_guard_positions_mark_brienne_oathkeeper_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = {
	$OWNER$ = { save_scope_as = owner }

	set_artifact_rarity_famed = yes

	create_artifact = {
		name = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_name
		description = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_description
		type = armor_plate
		visuals = $VISUALS$
		wealth = scope:wealth
		quality = scope:quality
		template = $TEMPLATE$
		modifier = $MODIFIER$
		save_scope_as = newly_created_artifact
		decaying = no
	}

	scope:newly_created_artifact = {
		set_variable = { name = $ARTIFACT_VARIABLE$ value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = historical_unique_artifact value = yes }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact value = yes }
		equip_artifact_to_owner_replace = yes
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_mark_new_guard_armor_artifact_effect = {
	scope:newly_created_artifact ?= {
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact value = yes }
		equip_artifact_to_owner_replace = yes
	}
}

# Gives service armour a stable character-and-order name regardless of whether
# it was created by a native AGOT armour effect or by our generic armour helper.
agot_guard_positions_name_guard_armor_effect = {
	save_scope_as = owner
	if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes }
		$ARTIFACT$ = { set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_armor_name }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes }
		$ARTIFACT$ = { set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_armor_name }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_kingsmen_trigger = yes }
		$ARTIFACT$ = { set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_armor_name }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes }
		$ARTIFACT$ = { set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_armor_name }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_swords_of_braavos_trigger = yes }
		$ARTIFACT$ = { set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_armor_name }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_greenhand_trigger = yes }
		$ARTIFACT$ = { set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_armor_name }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_queensguard_trigger = yes }
		$ARTIFACT$ = { set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_armor_name }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = {
	every_character_artifact = {
		limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact }
		destroy_artifact = this
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_portrait_armor_effect = {
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_westerlands_dynastic_armor
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_vale_dynastic_armor
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_dorne_dynastic_armor
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_valyrian_dynastic_armor
}

# De-duplicated entry point for "An Oath Fulfilled" after a non-death vacancy.
# Death already reaches the same AGOT vacancy event through the normal path.
agot_guard_positions_queue_vacancy_event_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_alive = yes
			is_ai = no
			agot_guard_positions_has_active_guard_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_review_pending }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vacancy_event_pending }
		}
		add_character_flag = { flag = agot_guard_positions_vacancy_event_pending days = 7 }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1100 days = 2 }
	}
}


agot_guard_positions_fire_custom_guard_councillor_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { $GUARD$ = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes } }
		$GUARD$ = { add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard }
		fire_councillor = $GUARD$
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_remove_order_bound_guard_service_effect = {
	save_scope_as = order_bound_guard
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:old_employer }
		scope:old_employer = { save_scope_as = guard_service_owner }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:councillor_liege }
		scope:councillor_liege = { save_scope_as = guard_service_owner }
	}
	add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:guard_service_owner }
		scope:guard_service_owner = {
			agot_guard_positions_fire_custom_guard_councillor_effect = { GUARD = scope:order_bound_guard }
		}
	}
	agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
	agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_portrait_armor_effect = yes
	remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member
	remove_trait = agot_queensguard
	remove_trait = agot_battleguard
	remove_trait = agot_winged_knight
	remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
	remove_trait = agot_kingsmen
	remove_trait = agot_greenhand
	remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_guard_ambition
	remove_character_flag = kingsguard_ambition
	remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:guard_service_owner }
		scope:guard_service_owner = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_repair_queued } }
				add_character_flag = { flag = agot_guard_positions_repair_queued days = 2 }
				trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1004 days = 1 }
			}
			agot_guard_positions_queue_vacancy_event_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_cleanup_order_bound_guard_councillors_effect = {
	every_councillor = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_is_order_bound_guard_trigger = yes
			NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_remove_order_bound_guard_service_effect = yes
	}
	every_courtier_or_guest = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_is_order_bound_guard_trigger = yes
			NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_remove_order_bound_guard_service_effect = yes
	}
	every_vassal_or_below = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_is_order_bound_guard_trigger = yes
			NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_remove_order_bound_guard_service_effect = yes
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_clear_rainbow_guard_armor_commission_effect = {
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_armor_remaining_1
	remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_armor_style
	remove_variable = selected_artifact
	clear_variable_list = possible_artifacts
}

agot_guard_positions_refund_remaining_rainbow_guard_armor_commissions_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_armor_remaining_1 }
		add_gold = agot_special_commission_cost
	}
	agot_guard_positions_clear_rainbow_guard_armor_commission_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_positions_select_rainbow_guard_armor_style_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_armor_style value = flag:$STYLE$ }
	trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1200 }
}

agot_guard_positions_add_current_artifact_to_armor_picker_effect = {
	add_to_variable_list = {
		name = possible_artifacts
		target = scope:newly_created_artifact
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = selected_artifact } }
		set_variable = { name = selected_artifact value = scope:newly_created_artifact }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_apply_selected_rainbow_guard_armor_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:smith } }
		random_court_position_holder = {
			type = court_smith_court_position
			save_scope_as = smith
		}
	}
	$RECIPIENT$ = {
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_rainbow_armor_style = flag:baratheon_armor_01 } }
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = { OWNER = this VISUALS = baratheon_armor_01_visuals TEMPLATE = agot_guard_positions_sworn_equipment_template MODIFIER = baratheon_armor_01_modifier ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = baratheon_armor_01_artifact }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_rainbow_armor_style = flag:stormlander_armor_02 } }
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = { OWNER = this VISUALS = stormlander_armor_02_visuals TEMPLATE = agot_guard_positions_sworn_equipment_template MODIFIER = stormlander_armor_02_modifier ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = stormlander_armor_02_artifact }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_rainbow_armor_style = flag:stormlander_armor_03 } }
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = { OWNER = this VISUALS = stormlander_armor_03_visuals TEMPLATE = agot_guard_positions_sworn_equipment_template MODIFIER = stormlander_armor_03_modifier ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = stormlander_armor_03_artifact }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_rainbow_armor_style = flag:tyrell_armor_01 } }
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = { OWNER = this VISUALS = tyrell_armor_01_visuals TEMPLATE = agot_guard_positions_sworn_equipment_template MODIFIER = tyrell_armor_01_modifier ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = tyrell_armor_01_artifact }

			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_rainbow_armor_style = flag:rowan_armor_01 } }

		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_rainbow_armor_style = flag:osgrey_armor_01 } }
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = { OWNER = this VISUALS = osgrey_armor_01_visuals TEMPLATE = agot_guard_positions_sworn_equipment_template MODIFIER = osgrey_armor_01_modifier ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = osgrey_armor_01_artifact }

			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = { OWNER = this VISUALS = osgrey_armor_02_visuals TEMPLATE = agot_guard_positions_sworn_equipment_template MODIFIER = osgrey_armor_02_modifier ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = osgrey_armor_02_artifact }

agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_valyrian_steel_sword_commission_effect = {
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_cost_5000
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_cost_10000
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_cost_15000
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_weapon_custom_name
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_weapon_pending_custom_name
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_weapon_custom_name_confirmed
	remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order
	remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style
	remove_variable = custom_artifact_name
	remove_variable = selected_artifact
	clear_variable_list = possible_artifacts
}

agot_guard_positions_refund_guard_valyrian_steel_sword_commission_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_cost_5000 }
		add_gold = 5000
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_cost_10000 }
		add_gold = 10000
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_cost_15000 }
		add_gold = 15000
	}
	agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_valyrian_steel_sword_commission_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_positions_select_guard_valyrian_steel_sword_style_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style value = flag:$STYLE$ }
	agot_guard_positions_open_guard_valyrian_steel_member_picker_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_positions_choose_guard_valyrian_steel_sword_style_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style value = flag:$STYLE$ }
	trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1306 }
}

agot_guard_positions_open_guard_valyrian_steel_member_picker_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:battleguard }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1302 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:winged_knight }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1309 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:rainbow_guard }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1303 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:kingsmen }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1304 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:greenhand }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1308 }
	}
	else = {
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1301 }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_apply_or_name_selected_guard_valyrian_steel_sword_effect = {
	$RECIPIENT$ = { save_scope_as = agot_guard_positions_guard_weapon_recipient }
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_weapon_custom_name }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1307 }
	}
	else = {
		agot_guard_positions_apply_selected_guard_valyrian_steel_sword_effect = { RECIPIENT = scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_weapon_recipient }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_select_guard_valyrian_steel_order_effect = {
	remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order value = flag:$GUARD$ }
	trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1305 }
}

agot_guard_positions_select_or_choose_guard_valyrian_steel_order_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_winged_knights }
		agot_guard_positions_select_guard_valyrian_steel_order_effect = { GUARD = winged_knight }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_battleguard }
		agot_guard_positions_select_guard_valyrian_steel_order_effect = { GUARD = battleguard }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard }
		agot_guard_positions_select_guard_valyrian_steel_order_effect = { GUARD = rainbow_guard }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_kingsmen }
		agot_guard_positions_select_guard_valyrian_steel_order_effect = { GUARD = kingsmen }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_has_greenhand_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_select_guard_valyrian_steel_order_effect = { GUARD = greenhand }
	}
	else = {
		# Defensive fallback for legacy saves or an invalid multi-order state.
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1301 }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_add_current_artifact_to_valyrian_steel_weapon_picker_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:newly_created_artifact }
		scope:newly_created_artifact = {
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_vs_weapon_picker_artifact value = yes }
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_vs_weapon_picker_style value = flag:$STYLE$ }
		}
		add_to_variable_list = {
			name = possible_artifacts
			target = scope:newly_created_artifact
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = selected_artifact } }
			set_variable = { name = selected_artifact value = scope:newly_created_artifact }
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_build_guard_valyrian_steel_weapon_picker_effect = {
	title:c_ruins.holder = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				root = {
					var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:battleguard
					has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_cost_5000
				}
			}
			agot_create_artifact_vs_firstman_armingsword_silver_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
			root = { agot_guard_positions_add_current_artifact_to_valyrian_steel_weapon_picker_effect = { STYLE = firstman_sword } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				root = {
					var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:battleguard

				}
			}
			agot_create_artifact_vs_firstman_armingsword_gold_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }

				}
			}
			agot_create_artifact_vs_firstman_longsword_gold_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
			root = { agot_guard_positions_add_current_artifact_to_valyrian_steel_weapon_picker_effect = { STYLE = firstman_sword } }
		}
		create_artifact_vs_sword_1_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }

			limit = {

					OR = {
						has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_cost_10000

			root = { agot_guard_positions_add_current_artifact_to_valyrian_steel_weapon_picker_effect = { STYLE = vs_sword_5 } }
		}
		create_artifact_vs_dagger_1_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		root = { agot_guard_positions_add_current_artifact_to_valyrian_steel_weapon_picker_effect = { STYLE = vs_dagger_1 } }
		create_artifact_vs_spear_1_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		root = { agot_guard_positions_add_current_artifact_to_valyrian_steel_weapon_picker_effect = { STYLE = vs_spear_1 } }
		create_artifact_vs_mace_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		root = { agot_guard_positions_add_current_artifact_to_valyrian_steel_weapon_picker_effect = { STYLE = vs_mace_1 } }
		create_artifact_vs_axe_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		root = { agot_guard_positions_add_current_artifact_to_valyrian_steel_weapon_picker_effect = { STYLE = vs_axe } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				root = {
					OR = {
						has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_cost_10000
						has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_cost_15000

				}
