			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_22_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_22 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_22_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_23_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_23 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_23_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_24_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_24 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_24_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_25_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_25 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_25_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_26_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_26 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_26_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_27_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_27 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_27_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_28_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_28 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_28_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_29_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_29 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_29_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_30_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_30 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_30_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_31_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_31 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_31_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_32_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_32 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_32_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_33_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_33 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_33_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_34_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_34 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_34_used } }
			1 = { trigger = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_35_used } } } set_artifact_name = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_35 root = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_kingsmen_weapon_name_35_used } }
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_apply_selected_guard_valyrian_steel_sword_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:smith } }
		random_court_position_holder = {
			type = court_smith_court_position
			save_scope_as = smith
		}
	}
	$RECIPIENT$ = {
		save_scope_as = agot_guard_positions_guard_weapon_recipient
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_sword_artifacts_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style = flag:firstman_sword } }
			if = {
				limit = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_cost_5000 } }
				agot_create_artifact_vs_firstman_armingsword_silver_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_cost_10000 } }
				agot_create_artifact_vs_firstman_armingsword_gold_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
			}
			else = {
				agot_create_artifact_vs_firstman_longsword_gold_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style = flag:vs_sword_1 } }
			create_artifact_vs_sword_1_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style = flag:vs_sword_2 } }
			create_artifact_vs_sword_2_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style = flag:vs_sword_3 } }
			create_artifact_vs_sword_3_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style = flag:vs_sword_4 } }
			create_artifact_vs_sword_4_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style = flag:vs_sword_5 } }
			create_artifact_vs_sword_5_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style = flag:vs_dagger_1 } }
			create_artifact_vs_dagger_1_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style = flag:vs_spear_1 } }
			create_artifact_vs_spear_1_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style = flag:vs_halberd } }
			create_artifact_vs_halberd_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style = flag:vs_mace_1 } }
			create_artifact_vs_mace_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style = flag:vs_mace_2 } }
			create_artifact_vs_mace_2_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_sword_style = flag:vs_axe } }
			create_artifact_vs_axe_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		}
		else = {
			create_artifact_vs_sword_1_basic_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = scope:smith }
		}
		scope:newly_created_artifact ?= {
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_valyrian_steel_guard_sword_artifact value = yes }
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_valyrian_steel_guard_weapon_artifact value = yes }
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_commissioner value = root }
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_recipient value = scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_weapon_recipient }
			if = {
				limit = { root = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_weapon_pending_custom_name } } }
				if = {
					limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:greenhand } }
					agot_guard_positions_name_greenhand_valyrian_steel_weapon_effect = yes
				}
				else = { agot_guard_positions_name_guard_valyrian_steel_weapon_effect = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:battleguard } }
				set_artifact_description = agot_guard_positions_guard_valyrian_steel_weapon_battleguard_desc
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:winged_knight } }
				set_artifact_description = agot_guard_positions_guard_valyrian_steel_weapon_winged_knight_desc
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:rainbow_guard } }
				set_artifact_description = agot_guard_positions_guard_valyrian_steel_weapon_rainbow_guard_desc
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:kingsmen } }
				set_artifact_description = agot_guard_positions_guard_valyrian_steel_weapon_kingsmen_desc
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { root = { var:agot_guard_positions_vs_guard_order = flag:greenhand } }
				set_artifact_description = agot_guard_positions_guard_valyrian_steel_weapon_greenhand_desc
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_vs_weapon_pending_custom_name } }
		agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_valyrian_steel_sword_commission_effect = yes
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_armor_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_is_swords_of_braavos_trigger = yes
			NOT = { any_character_artifact = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_armor_artifact has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact } }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = {
			OWNER = this
			VISUALS = braavosi_armor_01_visuals
			TEMPLATE = agot_guard_positions_sworn_equipment_template
			MODIFIER = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_armor_modifier
			ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_armor_artifact
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes
			NOT = { any_character_artifact = { has_variable = qohor_armor_02_artifact has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact } }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		agot_create_artifact_qohor_armor_02_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = this }
		agot_guard_positions_mark_new_guard_armor_artifact_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes
			NOT = { any_character_artifact = { has_variable = ned_armor_01_artifact has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact } }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		agot_create_artifact_ned_armor_01_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = this }
		agot_guard_positions_mark_new_guard_armor_artifact_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_is_kingsmen_trigger = yes
			liege_or_court_owner ?= { dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Baratheon }
			NOT = { any_character_artifact = { has_variable = stormlander_armor_03_artifact has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact } }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		agot_create_artifact_stormlander_armor_03_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = this }
		agot_guard_positions_mark_new_guard_armor_artifact_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_character_artifact = {
				has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact
			}
		}
		random_character_artifact = {
			limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact }
			save_scope_as = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor
		}
		if = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_greenhand_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_armor ?= {
				remove_variable = royce_armor_01_artifact
				set_variable = { name = reachman_armor_03_artifact value = yes }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_armor ?= { set_variable = { name = arryn_armor_01_artifact value = yes } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes }
			scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_armor ?= { set_variable = { name = ned_armor_01_artifact value = yes } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_kingsmen_trigger = yes liege_or_court_owner ?= { dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Baratheon } }
			scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_armor ?= { set_variable = { name = stormlander_armor_03_artifact value = yes } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes
			}
			scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_armor ?= { set_variable = { name = qohor_armor_02_artifact value = yes } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Tyrell
			}
			scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_armor ?= { set_variable = { name = tyrell_armor_01_artifact value = yes } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Lannister
			}
			scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_armor ?= { set_variable = { name = westerlander_armor_03_artifact value = yes } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes
				house ?= dynasty:dynn_Stark.dynasty_founder.house
			}
			scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_armor ?= { set_variable = { name = northman_armor_03_artifact value = yes } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Targaryen
			}
			scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_armor ?= { set_variable = { name = rhaegar_armor_01_artifact value = yes } }
		}
		else = {
			scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_armor ?= { set_variable = { name = royce_armor_01_artifact value = yes } }
		}
		scope:agot_guard_positions_guard_armor ?= {
			equip_artifact_to_owner_replace = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_greenhand_trigger = yes }
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = {
				OWNER = this
				VISUALS = reachman_armor_03_visuals
				TEMPLATE = reachman_armor_03_template
				MODIFIER = reachman_armor_03_modifier
				ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = reachman_armor_03_artifact
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes }
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = {
				OWNER = this
				VISUALS = arryn_armor_01_visuals
				TEMPLATE = agot_guard_positions_sworn_equipment_template
				MODIFIER = arryn_armor_01_modifier
				ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = arryn_armor_01_artifact
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes }
			agot_create_artifact_ned_armor_01_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = this }
			scope:newly_created_artifact ?= {
				set_variable = { name = historical_unique_artifact value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact value = yes }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_kingsmen_trigger = yes liege_or_court_owner ?= { dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Baratheon } }
			agot_create_artifact_stormlander_armor_03_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = this }
			scope:newly_created_artifact ?= {
				set_variable = { name = historical_unique_artifact value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact value = yes }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_kingsmen_trigger = yes }
			agot_create_artifact_baratheon_armor_01_effect = { OWNER = this CREATOR = this }
			scope:newly_created_artifact ?= {
				set_variable = { name = historical_unique_artifact value = yes }
				set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact value = yes }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes
			}
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = {
				OWNER = this
				VISUALS = qohor_armor_02_visuals
				TEMPLATE = qohor_armor_02_template
				MODIFIER = qohor_armor_02_modifier
				ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = qohor_armor_02_artifact
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Tyrell
			}
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = {
				OWNER = this
				VISUALS = tyrell_armor_01_visuals
				TEMPLATE = tyrell_armor_01_template
				MODIFIER = tyrell_armor_01_modifier
				ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = tyrell_armor_01_artifact
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Lannister
			}
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = {
				OWNER = this
				VISUALS = westerlander_armor_03_visuals
				TEMPLATE = westerlander_armor_03_template
				MODIFIER = westerlander_armor_03_modifier
				ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = westerlander_armor_03_artifact
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes
				house ?= dynasty:dynn_Stark.dynasty_founder.house
			}
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = {
				OWNER = this
				VISUALS = northman_armor_03_visuals
				TEMPLATE = northman_armor_03_template
				MODIFIER = northman_armor_03_modifier
				ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = northman_armor_03_artifact
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Targaryen
			}
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = {
				OWNER = this
				VISUALS = rhaegar_armor_01_visuals
				TEMPLATE = rhaegar_armor_01_template
				MODIFIER = rhaegar_armor_01_modifier
				ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = rhaegar_armor_01_artifact
			}
		}
		else = {
			agot_guard_positions_create_guard_armor_effect = {
				OWNER = this
				VISUALS = royce_armor_01_visuals
				TEMPLATE = royce_armor_01_template
				MODIFIER = royce_armor_01_modifier
				ARTIFACT_VARIABLE = royce_armor_01_artifact
			}
		}
	}

	# Run after every creation/adoption path so native armour names such as
	# "Qohorik Common Armor" are replaced as well.
	random_character_artifact = {
		limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_armor_artifact }
		save_scope_as = agot_guard_positions_named_guard_armor
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:agot_guard_positions_named_guard_armor }
		agot_guard_positions_name_guard_armor_effect = { ARTIFACT = scope:agot_guard_positions_named_guard_armor }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = {
	agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_sword_effect = yes
	agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_armor_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_positions_transfer_guard_sword_to_house_head_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_character_artifact = {
				has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_sword_artifact
			}
			house.house_head ?= {
				is_alive = yes
			}
		}
		house.house_head = { save_scope_as = agot_guard_positions_sword_recipient }
		random_character_artifact = {
			limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_guard_sword_artifact }
			set_owner = scope:agot_guard_positions_sword_recipient
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_for_court_effect = {
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander } cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_1 } cp:rainbow_guard_1 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_2 } cp:rainbow_guard_2 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_3 } cp:rainbow_guard_3 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_4 } cp:rainbow_guard_4 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_5 } cp:rainbow_guard_5 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_6 } cp:rainbow_guard_6 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:battleguard_lord_commander } cp:battleguard_lord_commander = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:battleguard_1 } cp:battleguard_1 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:battleguard_2 } cp:battleguard_2 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:battleguard_3 } cp:battleguard_3 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:battleguard_4 } cp:battleguard_4 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:battleguard_5 } cp:battleguard_5 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:battleguard_6 } cp:battleguard_6 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsmen_lord_commander } cp:kingsmen_lord_commander = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsmen_1 } cp:kingsmen_1 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsmen_2 } cp:kingsmen_2 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsmen_3 } cp:kingsmen_3 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsmen_4 } cp:kingsmen_4 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsmen_5 } cp:kingsmen_5 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsmen_6 } cp:kingsmen_6 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:swords_of_braavos_lord_commander } cp:swords_of_braavos_lord_commander = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:swords_of_braavos_1 } cp:swords_of_braavos_1 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:swords_of_braavos_2 } cp:swords_of_braavos_2 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:swords_of_braavos_3 } cp:swords_of_braavos_3 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:swords_of_braavos_4 } cp:swords_of_braavos_4 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:swords_of_braavos_5 } cp:swords_of_braavos_5 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:swords_of_braavos_6 } cp:swords_of_braavos_6 = { agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes } }
}

agot_guard_positions_remove_duplicate_knight_trait_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = knight
			has_trait = squire
			OR = {
				has_trait = agot_rainbowguard
				has_trait = agot_kingsmen
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member
			}
		}
		remove_trait = squire
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_knight_guard_member_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			$GUARD$ = { agot_guard_positions_is_swords_of_braavos_trigger = yes }
		}
		# The Swords of Braavos are a knightless Braavosi order. Membership
		# never grants knighthood or Thaneship, though an existing knight is
		# not stripped merely for entering the order.
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			$GUARD$ = {
				agot_guard_positions_should_be_thane_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		$GUARD$ = {
			# The AGOT knight state is the completed knight track of the squire
			# trait. Northern guards receive Thaneship instead of that track.
			if = { limit = { has_trait = squire } remove_trait = squire }
			if = { limit = { has_trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire } remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire }
			if = { limit = { has_trait = knight } remove_trait = knight }
			add_trait = agot_thane
		}
	}
	else = {
		# Faith-of-the-Seven northerners (most visibly the Manderlys) are
		# knights. Remove a stale Thane trait from existing saves before applying
		# AGOT's native knighting lifecycle.
		$GUARD$ = {
			if = { limit = { has_trait = agot_thane } remove_trait = agot_thane }
			if = { limit = { has_trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire } remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { $GUARD$ = { is_agot_knight_trigger = no } }
			save_scope_as = agot_guard_positions_knight_maker
			agot_become_a_knight_effect = {
				KNIGHT_TO_BE = $GUARD$
				KNIGHT_MAKER = scope:agot_guard_positions_knight_maker
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_apply_standard_membership_effect = {
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_departure_cleanup_complete
	add_character_flag = $MEMBER_FLAG$
	add_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
	add_trait = $MEMBER_TRAIT$
}

agot_guard_on_get_position = {
	save_scope_as = agot_guard_positions_new_position_holder
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_departure_cleanup_complete
	got_council_position_effect = yes
	liege_or_court_owner ?= {
		agot_guard_positions_knight_guard_member_effect = { GUARD = scope:agot_guard_positions_new_position_holder }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_has_any_training_ambition_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_apply_standard_membership_effect = { MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_winged_knight }
		remove_trait = agot_battleguard
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		liege_or_court_owner = { add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_swords_of_braavos_trigger = yes }
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_departure_cleanup_complete
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_member
		add_trait = agot_swords_of_braavos
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		liege_or_court_owner = { add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_apply_standard_membership_effect = { MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_battleguard }
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		liege_or_court_owner = {
			add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_set_rainbow_guard_origin_armor_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_apply_standard_membership_effect = { MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_rainbowguard }
		agot_guard_positions_remove_duplicate_knight_trait_effect = yes
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		add_piety = minor_piety_gain
		liege_or_court_owner = {
			add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
			add_piety = minor_piety_gain
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_kingsmen_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_apply_standard_membership_effect = { MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_kingsmen }
		agot_guard_positions_remove_duplicate_knight_trait_effect = yes
		add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
		add_piety = minor_piety_gain
		liege_or_court_owner = {
			add_prestige = minor_prestige_gain
			add_piety = minor_piety_gain
		}
	}
	liege_or_court_owner = {
		agot_guard_positions_cleanup_orphan_guard_traits_effect = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_queensguard_trigger = yes
				agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes
				agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes
				agot_guard_positions_is_swords_of_braavos_trigger = yes
				agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes
				agot_guard_positions_is_kingsmen_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_handle_guard_title_inheritance_effect = {
	save_scope_as = inherited_guard
	if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:councillor_liege }
		scope:councillor_liege = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_1 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_2 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_3 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_4 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_5 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_6 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_1 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_2 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_3 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_4 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_5 }
						scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_6 }
					}
				}
				trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1100 days = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander } }
				fire_councillor = cp:battleguard_lord_commander
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_1 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:battleguard_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_2 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:battleguard_2
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_3 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:battleguard_3
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_4 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:battleguard_4
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_5 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:battleguard_5
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = battleguard_6 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:battleguard_6
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander } }
				fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_2
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_3
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_4
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_5
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_6
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander } }
				fire_councillor = cp:kingsmen_lord_commander
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_1 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:kingsmen_1
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_2 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:kingsmen_2
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_3 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:kingsmen_3
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_4 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:kingsmen_4
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_5 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:kingsmen_5
			}
			if = {
				limit = { scope:inherited_guard = { has_council_position = kingsmen_6 } }
				fire_councillor = cp:kingsmen_6
			}
		}
	}
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard
}

agot_guard_positions_strip_standard_membership_effect = {
	fired_from_council_position_effect = yes
	agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
	remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
	remove_character_flag = $MEMBER_FLAG$
	remove_trait = $MEMBER_TRAIT$
	remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard
}

agot_guard_on_lose_position = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_alive = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_commander_promotion_in_progress }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_departure_cleanup_complete }
		}
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_departure_cleanup_complete
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_strip_review_queued } }
			add_character_flag = { flag = agot_guard_positions_strip_review_queued days = 11 }
			trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1007 days = 10 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member has_trait = agot_battleguard } }
			agot_guard_positions_strip_standard_membership_effect = {
				MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
				MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_battleguard
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member has_trait = agot_winged_knight } }
			agot_guard_positions_strip_standard_membership_effect = {
				MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
				MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_winged_knight
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_member has_trait = agot_swords_of_braavos } }
			fired_from_council_position_effect = yes
			agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_member
			remove_trait = agot_swords_of_braavos
			remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		}
		if = { limit = { exists = scope:councillor_liege } scope:councillor_liege = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_repair_queued } } add_character_flag = { flag = agot_guard_positions_repair_queued days = 2 } trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1004 days = 1 } } } }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_on_lose_standard_order_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_alive = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_commander_promotion_in_progress }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_departure_cleanup_complete }
		}
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_departure_cleanup_complete
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_strip_review_queued } }
			add_character_flag = { flag = agot_guard_positions_strip_review_queued days = 11 }
			trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1007 days = 10 }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_strip_standard_membership_effect = { MEMBER_FLAG = $MEMBER_FLAG$ MEMBER_TRAIT = $MEMBER_TRAIT$ }
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:councillor_liege }
			scope:councillor_liege = {
				agot_guard_positions_cleanup_orphan_guard_traits_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_repair_queued } }
					add_character_flag = { flag = agot_guard_positions_repair_queued days = 2 }
					trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1004 days = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_on_lose_queensguard_position_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_alive = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_commander_promotion_in_progress }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_departure_cleanup_complete }
		}
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_departure_cleanup_complete
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:councillor_liege }
			scope:councillor_liege = {
				if = { limit = { var:agot_queensguard_holder_lord_commander ?= root } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_lord_commander }
				if = { limit = { var:agot_queensguard_holder_1 ?= root } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_1 }
				if = { limit = { var:agot_queensguard_holder_2 ?= root } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_2 }
				if = { limit = { var:agot_queensguard_holder_3 ?= root } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_3 }
				if = { limit = { var:agot_queensguard_holder_4 ?= root } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_4 }
				if = { limit = { var:agot_queensguard_holder_5 ?= root } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_5 }
				if = { limit = { var:agot_queensguard_holder_6 ?= root } remove_variable = agot_queensguard_holder_6 }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_repair_queued } }
					add_character_flag = { flag = agot_guard_positions_repair_queued days = 2 }
					trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1004 days = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_strip_review_queued } }
			add_character_flag = { flag = agot_guard_positions_strip_review_queued days = 11 }
			trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1007 days = 10 }
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_on_get_queensguard_position_effect = {
	got_council_position_effect = yes
	liege_or_court_owner ?= {
		agot_guard_positions_knight_guard_member_effect = { GUARD = root }
	}
	agot_guard_positions_apply_standard_membership_effect = {
		MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member
		MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_queensguard
	}
	if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_has_any_training_ambition_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes
	}
	agot_guard_positions_remove_duplicate_knight_trait_effect = yes
	agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_on_lose_rainbow_guard_position = {
	agot_guard_positions_on_lose_standard_order_effect = { MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_rainbowguard }
}

agot_guard_on_lose_kingsmen_position = {
	agot_guard_positions_on_lose_standard_order_effect = { MEMBER_FLAG = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member MEMBER_TRAIT = agot_kingsmen }
}

agot_guard_positions_bookmarked_setup_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_start_date = 8299.9.12
			this = character:Stark_6
			has_title = title:e_the_north
		}
		title:e_the_north = { remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_disbanded }
		add_character_flag = has_battleguard
		agot_guard_positions_assign_canon_battleguard_effect = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_start_date = 8299.9.12
			this = character:Baratheon_3
			has_title = title:k_dragonstone
		}
		title:k_dragonstone = { remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_disbanded }
		add_character_flag = has_kingsmen
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_guard_slots_initializing
		agot_guard_positions_assign_canon_kingsmen_effect = yes
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1004 days = 1 }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1004 days = 3 }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1004 days = 7 }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1005 days = 3 }
	}
}

# Robert's Rebellion bookmark: Eddard begins with the six surviving northern
# companions who rode with him to the Tower of Joy. Howland is assigned first
# and therefore occupies the structural Lord Commander position. Eddard was the
# seventh companion, so the final sworn slot deliberately remains vacant.
agot_guard_positions_setup_282_battleguard_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_start_date = 8282.9.15
			this = character:Stark_3
			has_title = title:e_the_north
		}
		title:e_the_north = { remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_disbanded }
		add_character_flag = has_battleguard
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_guard_slots_initializing

		# In this bookmark Brynden remains Knight of the Bloody Gate rather than
		# being folded into Eddard's pre-established Battleguard.
		if = {
			limit = {
				character:Tully_9 = { is_alive = yes }
				title:c_the_bloody_gate = { NOT = { holder = character:Tully_9 } }
			}
			create_title_and_vassal_change = {
				type = granted
				save_scope_as = battleguard_282_bloody_gate_change
				add_claim_on_loss = no
			}
			title:c_the_bloody_gate = {
				change_title_holder = {
					holder = character:Tully_9
					change = scope:battleguard_282_bloody_gate_change
				}
			}
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:battleguard_282_bloody_gate_change
		}

		# Assignment order is authoritative: Howland becomes Lord Commander.
		if = { limit = { character:Reed_1 = { is_alive = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes } } } agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = character:Reed_1 } }
		if = { limit = { character:Dustin_1 = { is_alive = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes } } } agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = character:Dustin_1 } }
		if = { limit = { character:Glover_2 = { is_alive = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes } } } agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = character:Glover_2 } }
		if = { limit = { character:Cassel_2 = { is_alive = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes } } } agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = character:Cassel_2 } }
		if = { limit = { character:Wull_4 = { is_alive = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes } } } agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = character:Wull_4 } }
		if = { limit = { character:Ryswell_10 = { is_alive = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes } } } agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = character:Ryswell_10 } }

		# Eddard's five other sworn companions begin as his friends. Howland is
		# deliberately excluded from this block as requested.
		if = { limit = { character:Dustin_1 = { is_alive = yes } } set_relation_friend = { reason = friend_campaigned_together target = character:Dustin_1 } }
		if = { limit = { character:Glover_2 = { is_alive = yes } } set_relation_friend = { reason = friend_campaigned_together target = character:Glover_2 } }
		if = { limit = { character:Cassel_2 = { is_alive = yes } } set_relation_friend = { reason = friend_campaigned_together target = character:Cassel_2 } }
		if = { limit = { character:Wull_4 = { is_alive = yes } } set_relation_friend = { reason = friend_campaigned_together target = character:Wull_4 } }
		if = { limit = { character:Ryswell_10 = { is_alive = yes } } set_relation_friend = { reason = friend_campaigned_together target = character:Ryswell_10 } }

		# Apply the shared canon academy reservations immediately at bookmark start
		# instead of waiting for the first yearly training-maintenance pulse.
		agot_guard_positions_enroll_canon_minor_guards_effect = yes

		# Donnel Locke and Wendel Manderly have completed the academy path and
		# wait as qualified graduates for the remaining vacancy.
		agot_guard_positions_make_282_battleguard_graduate_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Locke_2 }
		agot_guard_positions_make_282_battleguard_graduate_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Manderly_8 }

		agot_guard_positions_repair_guard_assignments_effect = yes
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1004 days = 1 }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1004 days = 3 }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1004 days = 7 }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1005 days = 3 }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_make_282_battleguard_graduate_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			$TRAINEE$ = {
				is_alive = yes
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_any_guard_member_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		add_courtier ?= $TRAINEE$
		$TRAINEE$ = {
			if = { limit = { agot_guard_positions_has_any_training_ambition_trigger = yes } agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes }
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_owner value = root }
			root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_guard_positions_trainee_list target = prev } }
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit value = 100 }
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 3 }
			add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_passed_final_trials
			add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_battleguard
			add_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard
			agot_guard_positions_assign_regional_academy_house_effect = yes
			agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_sync_guard_flags_from_titles_effect = {
	# Repair saves started in the 182 AC bookmark while the old title-sync bug
	# was active.  The marker distinguishes the stale flag from an order the
	# player has genuinely founded, so a formed Battleguard survives reloads.
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_start_date >= 8182.1.1
			game_start_date < 8183.1.1
			has_title = title:e_the_north
			has_character_flag = has_battleguard
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_founded_by_decision }
		}
		remove_character_flag = has_battleguard
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_active_guard_trigger = yes }
			this = character:Targaryen_63
			has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rhaenyra_106_queensguard
			has_title = title:k_dragonstone
			NOT = { has_title = title:h_the_iron_throne }
		}
		remove_character_flag = has_kingsmen
		add_character_flag = has_queensguard
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_active_guard_trigger = yes }
			this = character:Baratheon_3
			NOT = { has_title = title:h_the_iron_throne }
			has_title = title:k_dragonstone
			title:k_dragonstone = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_disbanded } }
			OR = {
				has_title = title:e_the_stormlands
				has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard
			}
		}
		agot_guard_positions_stannis_kingsmen_replaces_other_guards_effect = yes
	}
	# Do not infer that the Battleguard has been founded merely from ownership
	# of e_the_north. Canon bookmark setup and title succession preserve an
	# existing order; otherwise the holder must use decision_battleguard.
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_active_guard_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { has_title = title:h_the_iron_throne }
			has_title = title:e_the_stormlands
			title:e_the_stormlands = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_disbanded } }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard }
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					this = character:Baratheon_3
					has_title = title:k_dragonstone
				}
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_active_guard_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { has_title = title:h_the_iron_throne }
			has_title = title:k_dragonstone
			NOT = { dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Targaryen }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rhaenyra_106_queensguard }
			title:k_dragonstone = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_disbanded } }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = has_kingsmen }
		}
		add_character_flag = has_kingsmen
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_active_guard_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { has_title = title:h_the_iron_throne }
			has_title = title:e_the_vale
			title:e_the_vale = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_disbanded } }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = has_winged_knights }
		}
		add_character_flag = has_winged_knights
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_stannis_kingsmen_replaces_other_guards_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_title = title:h_the_iron_throne }
		agot_guard_positions_disband_custom_guards_for_iron_throne_effect = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_title = title:h_the_iron_throne }
			exists = scope:previous_holder
			scope:previous_holder = { has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard }
		}
		scope:previous_holder = {
			agot_guard_positions_end_rainbow_guard_effect = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_title = title:h_the_iron_throne }
			has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard
		}
		agot_guard_positions_end_rainbow_guard_effect = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_title = title:h_the_iron_throne }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = has_kingsmen }
		}
		title:k_dragonstone = { remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_disbanded }
		title:e_the_stormlands = { set_variable = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_disbanded }
		add_character_flag = has_kingsmen
		agot_guard_positions_assign_canon_kingsmen_effect = yes
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_disband_custom_guards_for_iron_throne_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_queensguard } agot_guard_positions_dissolve_queensguard_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_battleguard } agot_guard_positions_end_battleguard_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_winged_knights } agot_guard_positions_end_winged_knight_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard } agot_guard_positions_end_rainbow_guard_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_kingsmen } agot_guard_positions_end_kingsmen_effect = yes }
	if = { limit = { agot_guard_positions_has_greenhand_trigger = yes } agot_guard_positions_end_greenhand_effect = yes }
}

agot_guard_positions_set_rainbow_guard_origin_armor_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Cuy
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Crane
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Fossoway
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Gothard
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Peake
				this = character:Cuy_3
				this = character:Crane_5
			}
		}
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_westerlands_dynastic_armor
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_vale_dynastic_armor
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_dorne_dynastic_armor
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_valyrian_dynastic_armor
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Caron
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Morrigen
				dynasty ?= dynasty:dynn_Tarth
				this = character:Caron_5
				this = character:Morrigen_4
				this = character:Tarth_6
			}
		}
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_westerlands_dynastic_armor
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_vale_dynastic_armor
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_dorne_dynastic_armor
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_valyrian_dynastic_armor
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_westerlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_vale_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_dorne_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_valyrian_dynastic_armor
			}
			OR = {
				primary_title ?= { target_is_de_jure_liege_or_above = title:e_the_reach }
				capital_county ?= { target_is_de_jure_liege_or_above = title:e_the_reach }
				any_liege_or_above = { has_title = title:e_the_reach }
				liege_or_court_owner = {
					OR = {
						has_title = title:e_the_reach
						capital_province = { geographical_region = world_westeros_the_reach }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_westerlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_vale_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_dorne_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_valyrian_dynastic_armor
			}
			OR = {
				primary_title ?= { target_is_de_jure_liege_or_above = title:e_the_stormlands }
				capital_county ?= { target_is_de_jure_liege_or_above = title:e_the_stormlands }
				any_liege_or_above = { has_title = title:e_the_stormlands }
				liege_or_court_owner = {
					OR = {
						has_title = title:e_the_stormlands
						capital_province = { geographical_region = world_westeros_the_stormlands }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_westerlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_vale_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_dorne_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_valyrian_dynastic_armor
			}
			OR = {
				primary_title ?= { target_is_de_jure_liege_or_above = title:e_the_westerlands }
				capital_county ?= { target_is_de_jure_liege_or_above = title:e_the_westerlands }
				any_liege_or_above = { has_title = title:e_the_westerlands }
				liege_or_court_owner = {
					OR = {
						has_title = title:e_the_westerlands
						capital_province = { geographical_region = world_westeros_the_westerlands }
					}
				}
				has_culture = culture:westerman_main
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_westerlands_dynastic_armor
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_westerlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_vale_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_dorne_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_valyrian_dynastic_armor
			}
			OR = {
				primary_title ?= { target_is_de_jure_liege_or_above = title:e_the_vale }
				capital_county ?= { target_is_de_jure_liege_or_above = title:e_the_vale }
				any_liege_or_above = { has_title = title:e_the_vale }
				liege_or_court_owner = {
					OR = {
						has_title = title:e_the_vale
						capital_province = { geographical_region = world_westeros_the_vale }
					}
				}
				has_culture = culture:valeman_main
				has_culture = culture:valeman_upper
				has_culture = culture:valeman_finger
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_vale_dynastic_armor
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_westerlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_vale_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_dorne_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_valyrian_dynastic_armor
			}
			OR = {
				primary_title ?= { target_is_de_jure_liege_or_above = title:e_dorne }
				capital_county ?= { target_is_de_jure_liege_or_above = title:e_dorne }
				any_liege_or_above = { has_title = title:e_dorne }
				liege_or_court_owner = {
					OR = {
						has_title = title:e_dorne
						capital_province = { geographical_region = world_westeros_dorne }
					}
				}
				has_culture = culture:stone_dornish
				has_culture = culture:sand_dornish
				has_culture = culture:salt_dornish
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_dorne_dynastic_armor
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_westerlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_vale_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_dorne_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_valyrian_dynastic_armor
			}
			OR = {
				has_culture = culture:high_valyrian
				has_culture = culture:westerosi_valyrian
				has_culture = culture:essosi_valyrian
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_valyrian_dynastic_armor
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_westerlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_vale_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_dorne_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_valyrian_dynastic_armor
			}
			OR = {
				has_culture = culture:stormlander_main
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_stormlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_westerlands_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_vale_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_dorne_dynastic_armor
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_valyrian_dynastic_armor
			}
			OR = {
				has_culture = culture:reachman
				has_culture = culture:vineman
				has_culture = culture:honeywiner
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_origin_reach_dynastic_armor
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_refresh_rainbow_guard_origin_armor_for_court_effect = {
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander } cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander = { agot_guard_positions_set_rainbow_guard_origin_armor_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_1 } cp:rainbow_guard_1 = { agot_guard_positions_set_rainbow_guard_origin_armor_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_2 } cp:rainbow_guard_2 = { agot_guard_positions_set_rainbow_guard_origin_armor_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_3 } cp:rainbow_guard_3 = { agot_guard_positions_set_rainbow_guard_origin_armor_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_4 } cp:rainbow_guard_4 = { agot_guard_positions_set_rainbow_guard_origin_armor_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_5 } cp:rainbow_guard_5 = { agot_guard_positions_set_rainbow_guard_origin_armor_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_6 } cp:rainbow_guard_6 = { agot_guard_positions_set_rainbow_guard_origin_armor_effect = yes } }
}

agot_guard_positions_assign_active_guard_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_has_greenhand_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_assign_greenhand_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_queensguard }
		agot_guard_positions_employ_queensguard_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = has_battleguard
				has_title = title:e_the_north
			}
		}
		agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_winged_knights }
		agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = has_kingsmen
				has_title = title:k_dragonstone
			}
		}
		agot_guard_assign_kingsmen_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard
				has_title = title:e_the_stormlands
			}
		}
		agot_guard_assign_rainbow_guard_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ }
	}
}

		agot_guard_positions_knight_guard_member_effect = { GUARD = cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander }
		cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member add_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving add_trait = agot_rainbowguard agot_guard_positions_remove_duplicate_knight_trait_effect = yes }

	every_councillor = {

		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_strip_orphaned_guard_membership_if_no_position_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_alive = yes
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member
				has_trait = agot_queensguard
			}
			NOR = {
				has_council_position = queensguard_lord_commander
				has_council_position = queensguard_1
				has_council_position = queensguard_2
				has_council_position = queensguard_3
				has_council_position = queensguard_4
				has_council_position = queensguard_5
				has_council_position = queensguard_6
			}
		}
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_portrait_armor_effect = yes
		remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
		remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member
		remove_trait = agot_queensguard
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_alive = yes
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
				has_trait = agot_battleguard
			}
			NOR = {
				has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander
				has_council_position = battleguard_1
				has_council_position = battleguard_2
				has_council_position = battleguard_3
				has_council_position = battleguard_4
				has_council_position = battleguard_5
				has_council_position = battleguard_6
			}
		}
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_portrait_armor_effect = yes
		remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
		remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
		remove_trait = agot_battleguard
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_alive = yes
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
				has_trait = agot_winged_knight
			}
			NOR = {
				has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
				has_council_position = winged_knight_1
				has_council_position = winged_knight_2
				has_council_position = winged_knight_3
				has_council_position = winged_knight_4
				has_council_position = winged_knight_5
				has_council_position = winged_knight_6
			}
		}
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_portrait_armor_effect = yes
		remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
		remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
		remove_trait = agot_winged_knight
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_alive = yes
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
				has_trait = agot_rainbowguard
			}
			NOR = {
				has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
				has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
				has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
				has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
				has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
				has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
				has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6
			}
		}
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_portrait_armor_effect = yes
		remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
		remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
		remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_alive = yes
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member
				has_trait = agot_kingsmen
			}
			NOR = {
				has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander
				has_council_position = kingsmen_1
				has_council_position = kingsmen_2
				has_council_position = kingsmen_3
				has_council_position = kingsmen_4
				has_council_position = kingsmen_5
				has_council_position = kingsmen_6
			}
		}
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_portrait_armor_effect = yes
		remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
		remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member
		remove_trait = agot_kingsmen
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_alive = yes

				has_trait = agot_greenhand

				has_council_position = greenhand_2
				has_council_position = greenhand_3
				has_council_position = greenhand_4
				has_council_position = greenhand_5
				has_council_position = greenhand_6
			}
		}
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_portrait_armor_effect = yes
		remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
		remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member
		remove_trait = agot_greenhand
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_cleanup_orphan_guard_traits_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_character_flag = has_battleguard
			NOT = { agot_guard_has_empty_battleguard_slot_trigger = yes }
		}
		every_courtier_or_guest = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
					has_trait = agot_battleguard
				}
				NOR = {
					has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander
					has_council_position = battleguard_1
					has_council_position = battleguard_2
					has_council_position = battleguard_3
					has_council_position = battleguard_4
					has_council_position = battleguard_5
					has_council_position = battleguard_6
				}
			}
			agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
			remove_trait = agot_battleguard
		}
		every_vassal_or_below = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
					has_trait = agot_battleguard
				}
				NOR = {
					has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander
					has_council_position = battleguard_1
					has_council_position = battleguard_2
					has_council_position = battleguard_3
					has_council_position = battleguard_4
					has_council_position = battleguard_5
					has_council_position = battleguard_6
				}
			}
			agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
			remove_trait = agot_battleguard
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_character_flag = has_winged_knights
			NOT = { agot_guard_has_empty_winged_knight_slot_trigger = yes }
		}
		every_courtier_or_guest = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
					has_trait = agot_winged_knight
				}
				NOR = {
					has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
					has_council_position = winged_knight_1
					has_council_position = winged_knight_2
					has_council_position = winged_knight_3
					has_council_position = winged_knight_4
					has_council_position = winged_knight_5
					has_council_position = winged_knight_6
				}
			}
			agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
			remove_trait = agot_winged_knight
		}
		every_vassal_or_below = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
					has_trait = agot_winged_knight
				}
				NOR = {
					has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
					has_council_position = winged_knight_1
					has_council_position = winged_knight_2
					has_council_position = winged_knight_3
					has_council_position = winged_knight_4
					has_council_position = winged_knight_5
					has_council_position = winged_knight_6
				}
			}
			agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member

agot_guard_positions_assign_canon_battleguard_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { this = character:Stark_3 this = character:Stark_6 }
			has_character_flag = has_battleguard
			agot_guard_has_empty_battleguard_slot_trigger = yes
			character:Tully_9 = {
				is_alive = yes
				is_capable_adult = yes
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		# Brynden is appointed first so the structural commander slot,
		# battleguard_lord_commander, belongs to him from the order's creation.
		if = {
			limit = {
				title:c_the_bloody_gate = { holder = character:Tully_9 }
				character:Waynwood_19 = { is_alive = yes }
			}
			create_title_and_vassal_change = {
				type = granted
				save_scope_as = battleguard_canon_bloody_gate_change
				add_claim_on_loss = no
			}
			title:c_the_bloody_gate = {
				change_title_holder = {
					holder = character:Waynwood_19
					change = scope:battleguard_canon_bloody_gate_change
				}
			}
			resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:battleguard_canon_bloody_gate_change
		}
		agot_guard_positions_transfer_unlanded_guard_to_holder_effect = { GUARD = character:Tully_9 }
		agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = character:Tully_9 }
	}
	if = {

			agot_guard_has_empty_battleguard_slot_trigger = yes

agot_guard_positions_end_rainbow_guard_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander }
		cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander = {
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
			remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
			add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		}
		fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_1 }
		cp:rainbow_guard_1 = {
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
			remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
			add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		}
		fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_2 }
		cp:rainbow_guard_2 = {
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
			remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
			add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		}
		fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_2
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_3 }
		cp:rainbow_guard_3 = {
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
			remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
			add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		}
		fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_3
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_4 }
		cp:rainbow_guard_4 = {
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
			remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
			add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		}
		fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_4
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_5 }
		cp:rainbow_guard_5 = {
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
			remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
			add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		}
		fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_5
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_6 }
		cp:rainbow_guard_6 = {
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
			remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
			add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		}
		fire_councillor = cp:rainbow_guard_6
	}
	every_courtier_or_guest = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
		remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
	}
	every_vassal_or_below = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
		remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
	}
	every_vassal_or_below = {
		every_courtier_or_guest = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes }
			agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
			remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
		}
	}
	title:e_the_stormlands = { set_variable = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_disbanded }
	remove_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard

	# Disbanding the order also ends every active academy enrolment for it.
	every_living_character = {
		limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard }
		agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_end_battleguard_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:battleguard_lord_commander }

			remove_trait = agot_battleguard
		}

	# Succession and conquest can strand former guards outside the new holder's
	# court or realm. Recover marked members for every guard order. Gated on
	# having anywhere to put a recovered guard: a world scan is pointless if
	# every slot this owner runs is already full.
