		limit = { agot_guard_positions_has_empty_active_guard_slot_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_recover_stranded_guards_effect = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		agot_guard_positions_ai_fill_guard_assignments_effect = yes
	}
	agot_guard_positions_cleanup_orphan_guard_traits_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_positions_clear_inheritance_review_state_effect = {
	every_in_list = { variable = inherited_guard_review_list remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_reviewed remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_lc_slot_saved }
	every_in_list = { variable = inherited_guard_keep_list remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_reviewed remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_lc_slot_saved }
	clear_variable_list = inherited_guard_review_list
	clear_variable_list = inherited_guard_keep_list
	clear_variable_list = inherited_guard_refusal_list
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_review_pending
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_battleguard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_rainbow_guard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_kingsmen
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_winged_knights
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_swords_of_braavos
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_greenhand
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_queensguard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_native_kingsguard
}

agot_guard_positions_mark_guard_transition_order_effect = {
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_battleguard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_rainbow_guard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_kingsmen
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_winged_knights
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_greenhand
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_queensguard
	if = { limit = { scope:guard_transition_title = { this = title:e_the_north } } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_battleguard }
	else_if = { limit = { scope:guard_transition_title = { this = title:e_the_stormlands } } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_rainbow_guard }
	else_if = { limit = { scope:guard_transition_title = { this = title:k_dragonstone } } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_kingsmen }
	else_if = { limit = { scope:guard_transition_title = { this = title:e_the_vale } } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_winged_knights }
	else_if = { limit = { scope:guard_transition_title = { this = title:k_braavos } } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_swords_of_braavos }
	else_if = { limit = { scope:guard_transition_title = { this = title:d_highgarden } } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_greenhand }
}

# Rebuild the inherited roster without assigning a Lord Commander. Player-kept
# guards are held in a list until the holder explicitly chooses the commander.
agot_guard_positions_prepare_guard_transition_review_effect = {
	scope:previous_holder ?= { save_scope_as = previous_guard_holder }
	clear_variable_list = inherited_guard_review_list
	clear_variable_list = inherited_guard_keep_list
	clear_variable_list = inherited_guard_refusal_list
	add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_review_pending
	agot_guard_positions_mark_guard_transition_order_effect = yes

	# For a player inheritance, reserve the commander slot until the holder chooses
	# who will lead the retained guard. The inherited-title flag authorizes the
	# temporary council removal without treating it as dismissal.
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = no }
		if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_battleguard exists = cp:battleguard_lord_commander } cp:battleguard_lord_commander = { save_scope_as = inherited_original_commander add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard } fire_councillor = scope:inherited_original_commander scope:inherited_original_commander = { remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_rainbow_guard exists = cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander } cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander = { save_scope_as = inherited_original_commander add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard } fire_councillor = scope:inherited_original_commander scope:inherited_original_commander = { remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_kingsmen exists = cp:kingsmen_lord_commander } cp:kingsmen_lord_commander = { save_scope_as = inherited_original_commander add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard } fire_councillor = scope:inherited_original_commander scope:inherited_original_commander = { remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_winged_knights exists = cp:winged_knight_lord_commander } cp:winged_knight_lord_commander = { save_scope_as = inherited_original_commander add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard } fire_councillor = scope:inherited_original_commander scope:inherited_original_commander = { remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_swords_of_braavos exists = cp:swords_of_braavos_lord_commander } cp:swords_of_braavos_lord_commander = { save_scope_as = inherited_original_commander add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard } fire_councillor = scope:inherited_original_commander scope:inherited_original_commander = { remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_greenhand exists = cp:greenhand_lord_commander } cp:greenhand_lord_commander = { save_scope_as = inherited_original_commander add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard } fire_councillor = scope:inherited_original_commander scope:inherited_original_commander = { remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard } }
	}

	every_living_character = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { this = root }
			OR = {
				AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_battleguard } has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member }
				AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_rainbow_guard } has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member }
				AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_kingsmen } has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member }
				AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_winged_knights } has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member }
				AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_swords_of_braavos } has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_member }
				AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_greenhand } has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_landed = yes NOT = { liege = root } }
			root = { agot_guard_positions_dismiss_custom_guard_member_effect = { GUARD = prev } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				root = { is_ai = no }
				has_variable = agot_guard_positions_oath_holder
				var:agot_guard_positions_oath_holder ?= scope:previous_holder
				var:agot_guard_positions_loyalty >= 75
				scope:previous_holder = { is_alive = yes }
			}
			root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = inherited_guard_refusal_list target = prev } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { root = { is_ai = yes } }
			root = { agot_guard_positions_keep_inherited_guard_member_effect = { GUARD = prev } }
		}
		else = { root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = inherited_guard_review_list target = prev } } }
	}

	# Trainees are not council members and are invisible to the member-review
	# loop above. Re-parent them to the new holder unconditionally (not part of
	# the keep/dismiss/refuse decision) so the academy roster and its
	# maintenance pulse do not silently lose track of them after succession.
	every_living_character = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { this = root }
			has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_owner
			var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner ?= scope:previous_holder
			OR = {
				AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_battleguard } has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard }
				AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_rainbow_guard } has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard }
				AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_kingsmen } has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen }
				AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_winged_knights } has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights }
				AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_greenhand } has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand }
			}
		}
		var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner = { remove_list_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_trainee_list target = prev } }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_owner value = root }
		root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_guard_positions_trainee_list target = prev } }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_repair_guard_assignments_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_clear_inheritance_review_state_effect = yes
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_in_list = { variable = inherited_guard_refusal_list always = yes } }
			every_in_list = {
				variable = inherited_guard_refusal_list
				save_scope_as = refusing_inherited_guard
				root = { agot_guard_positions_dismiss_custom_guard_member_effect = { GUARD = scope:refusing_inherited_guard } }
				scope:refusing_inherited_guard = {
					if = { limit = { scope:previous_holder = { is_alive = yes } } set_employer = scope:previous_holder }
					add_opinion = { target = scope:previous_holder modifier = grateful_opinion opinion = 20 }
				}
			}
			trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1011 days = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { any_in_list = { variable = inherited_guard_review_list always = yes } }
			trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1010 days = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			agot_guard_positions_repair_guard_assignments_effect = yes
			agot_guard_positions_clear_inheritance_review_state_effect = yes
			agot_guard_positions_queue_vacancy_event_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# The native Kingsguard already transfers with the Iron Throne. Review that
# roster with the same player-facing flow rather than silently accepting it.
agot_guard_positions_prepare_native_kingsguard_review_effect = {
	clear_variable_list = inherited_guard_review_list
	clear_variable_list = inherited_guard_keep_list
	clear_variable_list = inherited_guard_refusal_list
	add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_review_pending
	add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_native_kingsguard
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsguard_lord_commander } add_to_variable_list = { name = inherited_guard_review_list target = cp:kingsguard_lord_commander } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsguard_1 } add_to_variable_list = { name = inherited_guard_review_list target = cp:kingsguard_1 } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsguard_2 } add_to_variable_list = { name = inherited_guard_review_list target = cp:kingsguard_2 } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsguard_3 } add_to_variable_list = { name = inherited_guard_review_list target = cp:kingsguard_3 } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsguard_4 } add_to_variable_list = { name = inherited_guard_review_list target = cp:kingsguard_4 } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsguard_5 } add_to_variable_list = { name = inherited_guard_review_list target = cp:kingsguard_5 } }
	if = { limit = { exists = cp:kingsguard_6 } add_to_variable_list = { name = inherited_guard_review_list target = cp:kingsguard_6 } }
	if = {
		limit = { any_in_list = { variable = inherited_guard_review_list always = yes } }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1010 days = 1 }
	}
	else = { agot_guard_positions_clear_inheritance_review_state_effect = yes }
}

# Finish a reviewed regional order. The chosen commander is assigned FIRST, so
# the dedicated office is genuinely the holder's choice rather than whichever
# guard happened to be processed first.
agot_guard_positions_finalize_custom_inherited_guard_effect = {
	$COMMANDER$ = { save_scope_as = chosen_inherited_commander }
	# If the chosen guard still occupies a non-command slot, vacate only that slot
	# under the inherited-title authorization. Membership/traits remain intact.
	scope:chosen_inherited_commander = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard }
	if = { limit = { scope:chosen_inherited_commander = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes } } fire_councillor = scope:chosen_inherited_commander }
	scope:chosen_inherited_commander = { remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard }
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_battleguard }
		assign_councillor_type = { type = battleguard_lord_commander target = scope:chosen_inherited_commander }
	}
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_rainbow_guard } assign_councillor_type = { type = rainbow_guard_lord_commander target = scope:chosen_inherited_commander } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_kingsmen } assign_councillor_type = { type = kingsmen_lord_commander target = scope:chosen_inherited_commander } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_winged_knights } assign_councillor_type = { type = winged_knight_lord_commander target = scope:chosen_inherited_commander } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_swords_of_braavos } assign_councillor_type = { type = swords_of_braavos_lord_commander target = scope:chosen_inherited_commander } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_greenhand } assign_councillor_type = { type = greenhand_lord_commander target = scope:chosen_inherited_commander } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inheritance_queensguard } assign_councillor_type = { type = queensguard_lord_commander target = scope:chosen_inherited_commander } }
	scope:chosen_inherited_commander = { agot_guard_positions_record_lord_commander_effect = { HOLDER = root } }
	every_in_list = {
		variable = inherited_guard_keep_list
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_oath_holder value = root }
		agot_guard_positions_update_guard_loyalty_effect = { HOLDER = root }
	}
	# The former commander was deliberately left positionless; the ordinary repair
	# pass restores that retained member and any other legitimate holes after the
	# chosen commander has secured the dedicated office.
	agot_guard_positions_repair_guard_assignments_effect = yes
	agot_guard_positions_clear_inheritance_review_state_effect = yes
	agot_guard_positions_queue_vacancy_event_effect = yes
}


# Native Kingsguard promotion only rotates the selected member and the previous
# Lord Commander; all other retained white cloaks stay untouched.
agot_guard_positions_finalize_native_inherited_kingsguard_effect = {
	$COMMANDER$ = { save_scope_as = chosen_inherited_commander }
	add_character_flag = { flag = agot_guard_positions_suppress_vacancy_events days = 5 }
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:kingsguard_lord_commander NOT = { cp:kingsguard_lord_commander = scope:chosen_inherited_commander } }
		cp:kingsguard_lord_commander = { save_scope_as = old_inherited_kingsguard_commander }
		agot_remove_kingsguard_effect = { KINGSGUARD = scope:chosen_inherited_commander }
		agot_remove_kingsguard_effect = { KINGSGUARD = scope:old_inherited_kingsguard_commander }
		agot_join_kingsguard_effect = { KINGSGUARD = scope:chosen_inherited_commander KING = root }
		if = { limit = { scope:old_inherited_kingsguard_commander = { is_alive = yes } } agot_join_kingsguard_effect = { KINGSGUARD = scope:old_inherited_kingsguard_commander KING = root } }
	}
	agot_guard_positions_clear_inheritance_review_state_effect = yes
	# If the player dismissed any inherited white cloaks, open the normal vacancy
	# flow after the short suppression window rather than spamming it mid-review.
	trigger_event = { id = agot_kingsguard.1001 days = 6 }
}

agot_guard_positions_dismiss_custom_guard_member_effect = {
	$GUARD$ = { save_scope_as = dismissed_guard }
	$GUARD$ = { employer ?= { save_scope_as = former_guard_holder } }
	# Record the former-order trait BEFORE firing the council position; successful
	# council cleanup removes live membership markers immediately.
	$GUARD$ = {
		if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member } remove_character_modifier = agot_former_queensguard add_trait = former_queensguard }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member } remove_character_modifier = agot_former_battleguard add_trait = former_battleguard }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member } remove_character_modifier = agot_former_winged_knight add_trait = former_winged_knight }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member } remove_character_modifier = agot_former_rainbow_guard add_trait = former_rainbow_guard }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member } remove_character_modifier = agot_former_kingsman add_trait = former_kingsman }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member } remove_character_modifier = agot_former_greenhand add_trait = former_greenhand }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_member } remove_character_modifier = agot_former_swords_of_braavos add_trait = former_swords_of_braavos }
		# Record while the live order marker still exists, so the correct
		# order-specific memory and chronicle entry are selected exactly once.
		agot_guard_positions_record_dismissal_effect = { HOLDER = scope:former_guard_holder }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { $GUARD$ = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes } }
		agot_guard_positions_fire_custom_guard_councillor_effect = { GUARD = scope:dismissed_guard }
	}
	$GUARD$ = {
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_service_weapon_artifacts_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_portrait_armor_effect = yes
		remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_member
		remove_trait = agot_queensguard
		remove_trait = agot_battleguard
		remove_trait = agot_winged_knight
		remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
		remove_trait = agot_kingsmen
		remove_trait = agot_greenhand
		remove_trait = agot_swords_of_braavos
		remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard
	}
	# A deliberate dismissal creates a real vacancy immediately. Do not rely on
	# the slower repair pulse to discover it.
	agot_guard_positions_queue_vacancy_event_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_positions_keep_inherited_guard_member_effect = {
	if = { limit = { scope:guard_transition_title = { this = title:e_the_north } } agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ } }
	else_if = { limit = { scope:guard_transition_title = { this = title:e_the_stormlands } } agot_guard_assign_rainbow_guard_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ } }
	else_if = { limit = { scope:guard_transition_title = { this = title:k_dragonstone } } agot_guard_assign_kingsmen_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ } }
	else_if = { limit = { scope:guard_transition_title = { this = title:e_the_vale } } agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ } }
	else_if = { limit = { scope:guard_transition_title = { this = title:k_braavos } } agot_guard_assign_swords_of_braavos_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ } }
	else_if = { limit = { scope:guard_transition_title = { this = title:d_highgarden } } agot_guard_assign_greenhand_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ } }
	# Death succession renews this silently; a guard retained after a living
	# transition renews it as part of the successor's explicit review.
	$GUARD$ = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_oath_holder value = root }
		agot_guard_positions_update_guard_loyalty_effect = { HOLDER = root }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_record_appointment_effect = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_appointments } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_appointments value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_dismissals } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_dismissals value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_mission_successes } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_mission_successes value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_mission_failures } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_mission_failures value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_commands } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_commands value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_rescues } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_rescues value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_criminals_captured } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_criminals_captured value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_wards_protected } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_wards_protected value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_tournament_victories } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_tournament_victories value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_battle_victories } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_battle_victories value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_wounds } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_wounds value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_duel_victories } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_duel_victories value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_protection_experience } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_protection_experience value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_pursuit_experience } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_pursuit_experience value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_investigation_experience } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_investigation_experience value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_infiltration_experience } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_infiltration_experience value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_ward_experience } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_ward_experience value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_battle_experience } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_battle_experience value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_instruction_experience } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_instruction_experience value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_experience } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_experience value = 0 } }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_chronicle_appointment_recorded } }
		if = { limit = { is_kingsguard_trigger = yes } create_character_memory = { type = agot_kingsguard_service_appointed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_rainbow_guard_appointed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_battleguard_appointed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_winged_knight_appointed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_swords_of_braavos_appointed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_kingsman_appointed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_queensguard_appointed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_greenhand_appointed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_chronicle_appointment_recorded
		change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_appointments add = 1 }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_oath_holder value = $HOLDER$ }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_loyalty value = 50 }
		agot_guard_order_book_create_page_effect = { HOLDER = $HOLDER$ }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_record_dismissal_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_honorable_retirement }
		create_character_memory = { type = agot_guard_service_honorable_retirement_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } }
		agot_guard_order_book_append_retirement_effect = { HOLDER = $HOLDER$ }
	}
	else = {
	if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_rainbow_guard_dismissed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_battleguard_dismissed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_winged_knight_dismissed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_swords_of_braavos_dismissed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_kingsman_dismissed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_queensguard_dismissed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_greenhand_dismissed_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	agot_guard_order_book_append_dismissal_effect = { HOLDER = $HOLDER$ }
	}
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_chronicle_appointment_recorded
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_dismissals add = 1 }
}

# Landed councillors serve from their own holdings. Transfer only unlanded
# candidates who are not already present at the guard holder's court.
agot_guard_positions_transfer_unlanded_guard_to_holder_effect = {
	$GUARD$ ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_landed = no
				is_ruler = no
				NOT = { is_courtier_of = root }
			}
			root = { add_courtier = $GUARD$ }
		}
	}
}

# Chronicle entries are written against the order the character actually serves.
# Keeping this dispatch here gives activities, missions and optional compatches one
# stable public hook without making them know every order's memory key.
agot_guard_positions_record_lord_commander_effect = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_commands } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_commands value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { is_kingsguard_trigger = yes } create_character_memory = { type = agot_kingsguard_service_lord_commander_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_rainbow_guard_lord_commander_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_battleguard_lord_commander_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_winged_knight_lord_commander_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_swords_of_braavos_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_swords_of_braavos_lord_commander_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_kingsman_lord_commander_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_queensguard_lord_commander_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_greenhand_lord_commander_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	agot_guard_order_book_append_lord_commander_effect = { HOLDER = $HOLDER$ }
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_commands add = 1 }
	add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_command_recorded
}

agot_guard_positions_record_rescue_effect = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_service_rescues } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_rescues value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { is_kingsguard_trigger = yes } create_character_memory = { type = agot_kingsguard_service_rescue_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_rainbow_guard_rescue_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_battleguard_rescue_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_winged_knight_rescue_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_kingsman_rescue_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_queensguard_rescue_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_greenhand_rescue_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	agot_guard_order_book_append_rescue_effect = { HOLDER = $HOLDER$ }
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_rescues add = 1 }
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_protection_experience add = 1 }
	agot_guard_positions_update_specialisation_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_positions_record_capture_effect = {
	agot_guard_order_book_append_capture_effect = yes
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_criminals_captured add = 1 }
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_pursuit_experience add = 1 }
	agot_guard_positions_update_specialisation_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_positions_record_ward_service_effect = {
	agot_guard_order_book_append_ward_effect = yes
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_wards_protected add = 1 }
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_ward_experience add = 1 }
	agot_guard_positions_update_specialisation_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_positions_record_tournament_victory_effect = {
	agot_guard_order_book_append_tournament_effect = yes
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_tournament_victories add = 1 }
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_battle_experience add = 1 }
	agot_guard_positions_update_specialisation_effect = yes
}

# Public, standalone-safe combat hook. Optional warfare mods can call the same
# effect, while the base on_combat_end_winner integration already works without
# any of them.
agot_guard_positions_record_battle_victory_effect = {
	if = { limit = { is_kingsguard_trigger = yes } create_character_memory = { type = agot_kingsguard_service_battle_victory_memory } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_rainbow_guard_battle_victory_memory } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_battleguard_battle_victory_memory } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_winged_knight_battle_victory_memory } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_kingsman_battle_victory_memory } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_queensguard_battle_victory_memory } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_greenhand_battle_victory_memory } }
	agot_guard_order_book_append_battle_effect = yes
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_battle_victories add = 1 }
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_battle_experience add = 1 }
	agot_guard_positions_update_specialisation_effect = yes
}

# Shared records are intentionally order-neutral: the character's appointment
# memories identify the book/order, while these entries remain callable from
# base combat and optional warfare or rescue mods without depending on them.
agot_guard_positions_record_wound_effect = {
	create_character_memory = { type = agot_guard_service_wounded_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } }
	agot_guard_order_book_append_wound_effect = { HOLDER = $HOLDER$ }
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_wounds add = 1 }
}

agot_guard_positions_record_duel_victory_effect = {
	create_character_memory = { type = agot_guard_service_duel_victory_memory participants = { duel_opponent = $OPPONENT$ } }
	agot_guard_order_book_append_duel_effect = { OPPONENT = $OPPONENT$ }
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_service_duel_victories add = 1 }
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_battle_experience add = 1 }
	agot_guard_positions_update_specialisation_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_positions_record_death_effect = {
	if = { limit = { is_kingsguard_trigger = yes } create_character_memory = { type = agot_kingsguard_service_death_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_rainbow_guard_death_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_battleguard_death_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_winged_knight_death_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_kingsman_death_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_queensguard_death_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member } create_character_memory = { type = agot_greenhand_death_memory participants = { guard_holder = $HOLDER$ } } }
	agot_guard_order_book_append_death_effect = { HOLDER = $HOLDER$ }
}

agot_guard_positions_update_specialisation_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_specialisation_trigger = yes } }
		if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_protection_experience >= 3 } add_trait = agot_guard_specialist_royal_protector }
		else_if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_pursuit_experience >= 3 } add_trait = agot_guard_specialist_pursuer }
		else_if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_investigation_experience >= 3 } add_trait = agot_guard_specialist_investigator }
		else_if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_infiltration_experience >= 3 } add_trait = agot_guard_specialist_infiltrator }
		else_if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_ward_experience >= 3 } add_trait = agot_guard_specialist_ward_protector }
		else_if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_battle_experience >= 3 } add_trait = agot_guard_specialist_battlefield_champion }
		else_if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_instruction_experience >= 3 } add_trait = agot_guard_specialist_academy_instructor }
		else_if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_training_experience >= 3 } add_trait = agot_guard_specialist_master_at_arms }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_clear_specialisation_effect = {
	remove_trait = agot_guard_specialist_royal_protector
	remove_trait = agot_guard_specialist_master_at_arms
	remove_trait = agot_guard_specialist_pursuer
	remove_trait = agot_guard_specialist_investigator
	remove_trait = agot_guard_specialist_infiltrator
	remove_trait = agot_guard_specialist_ward_protector
	remove_trait = agot_guard_specialist_battlefield_champion
	remove_trait = agot_guard_specialist_academy_instructor
}

agot_guard_positions_update_guard_loyalty_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_loyalty value = 50 }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = loyal } change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_loyalty add = 25 } }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = just } change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_loyalty add = 20 } }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = honest } change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_loyalty add = 10 } }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = content } change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_loyalty add = 10 } }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = deceitful } change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_loyalty subtract = 15 } }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = ambitious } change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_loyalty subtract = 10 } }
	if = { limit = { has_trait = arbitrary } change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_loyalty subtract = 10 } }
	if = { limit = { has_relation_friend = $HOLDER$ } change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_loyalty add = 25 } }
	if = { limit = { has_relation_lover = $HOLDER$ } change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_loyalty add = 35 } }
	if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_oath_holder ?= $HOLDER$ } change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_loyalty add = 15 } }
}

agot_guard_positions_update_realm_guard_loyalty_effect = {
	every_councillor = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_active_guard_member_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_chronicle_appointment_recorded } } agot_guard_positions_record_appointment_effect = { HOLDER = root } }
		# Existing saves may predate order-book pages while already carrying the
		# appointment marker. Backfill their persistent page without duplicating it.
		agot_guard_order_book_create_page_effect = { HOLDER = root }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_command_recorded }
				OR = {
					has_council_position = kingsguard_lord_commander
					has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
					has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
					has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander
					has_council_position = queensguard_lord_commander
					has_council_position = greenhand_lord_commander
				}
			}
			agot_guard_positions_record_lord_commander_effect = { HOLDER = root }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_update_guard_loyalty_effect = { HOLDER = root }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_recover_stranded_guards_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guard_owner
	every_living_character = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = stranded_guard
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member
				NOT = { has_council_position = queensguard_lord_commander }
				NOT = { has_council_position = queensguard_1 }
				NOT = { has_council_position = queensguard_2 }
				NOT = { has_council_position = queensguard_3 }
				NOT = { has_council_position = queensguard_4 }
				NOT = { has_council_position = queensguard_5 }
				NOT = { has_council_position = queensguard_6 }
				scope:guard_owner = { has_character_flag = has_queensguard }
			}
			scope:guard_owner = { agot_guard_positions_employ_queensguard_effect = { GUARD = scope:stranded_guard } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
				scope:guard_owner = { has_character_flag = has_battleguard }
			}
			scope:guard_owner = { agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = scope:stranded_guard } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
				scope:guard_owner = { has_character_flag = has_winged_knights }
			}
			scope:guard_owner = { agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = scope:stranded_guard } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
				scope:guard_owner = { has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard }
			}
			scope:guard_owner = { agot_guard_assign_rainbow_guard_effect = { GUARD = scope:stranded_guard } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
				scope:guard_owner = { has_character_flag = has_kingsmen }
			}
			scope:guard_owner = { agot_guard_assign_kingsmen_effect = { GUARD = scope:stranded_guard } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
				scope:guard_owner = { agot_guard_positions_has_greenhand_trigger = yes }
			}
			scope:guard_owner = { agot_guard_assign_greenhand_effect = { GUARD = scope:stranded_guard } }
		}
	}
}

# Death does not automatically remove character traits. This intentionally does
# not call the dismissal effect: death already vacates the council position and
# guard artifacts have their own inheritance/destruction handler.
agot_guard_positions_clear_deceased_guard_membership_effect = {
	# The sworn sword passes to the family in the earlier death on-action, but
	# office-bound guard armor ends with the service term.
	agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
	agot_guard_positions_clear_guard_portrait_armor_effect = yes
	remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
	remove_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_inherited_title_as_guard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_queensguard_member
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_member
	remove_trait = agot_queensguard
	remove_trait = agot_battleguard
	remove_trait = agot_winged_knight
	remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard
	remove_trait = agot_kingsmen
	remove_trait = agot_greenhand
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_on_mission
	remove_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_away_on_mission
	remove_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_mission_cooldown
	agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes
}

# Unlike generic recruitment, this pass does not impose a prowess threshold:
# being an adult, valid for the active order, and enrolled for that same order
# is the qualification. Merit still controls which trainee is selected first.
agot_guard_positions_ai_appoint_adult_trainee_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_has_empty_active_guard_slot_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_adult_trainee_fill_exhausted }
			NOT = {
				any_courtier_or_guest = {
					is_adult = yes
					agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root }
					agot_guard_positions_can_be_active_guard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
				}
			}
		}
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_adult_trainee_fill_exhausted
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_has_empty_active_guard_slot_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_adult_trainee_fill_exhausted }
			any_courtier_or_guest = {
				is_adult = yes
				agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root }
				agot_guard_positions_can_be_active_guard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
			}
		}
		ordered_courtier_or_guest = {
			position = 0
			order_by = {
				value = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit
				if = { limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_academy_graduate_trigger = yes } add = 10000 }
				add = prowess
				add = martial
			}
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root }
				agot_guard_positions_can_be_active_guard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
			}
			save_scope_as = ai_guard_trainee
		}
		scope:ai_guard_trainee = { agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_assign_active_guard_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_trainee }
	}
}

	agot_guard_positions_sync_guard_flags_from_titles_effect = yes
	agot_guard_positions_cleanup_order_bound_guard_councillors_effect = yes
	agot_guard_positions_debug_assign_existing_guard_members_effect = yes
	agot_guard_positions_refresh_rainbow_guard_origin_armor_for_court_effect = yes
	agot_guard_positions_cleanup_orphan_guard_traits_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_positions_debug_assign_existing_guard_members_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_battleguard }
		every_courtier_or_guest = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
					has_trait = agot_battleguard
					has_trait = agot_winged_knight
				}
				NOR = {
					has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander
					has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
					has_council_position = battleguard_1
					has_council_position = winged_knight_1
					has_council_position = battleguard_2
					has_council_position = winged_knight_2
					has_council_position = battleguard_3
					has_council_position = winged_knight_3
					has_council_position = battleguard_4
					has_council_position = winged_knight_4
					has_council_position = battleguard_5
					has_council_position = winged_knight_5
					has_council_position = battleguard_6
					has_council_position = winged_knight_6
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = guard_to_repair
			scope:guard_owner = {
				agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = scope:guard_to_repair }
			}
		}
		every_vassal_or_below = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
					has_trait = agot_battleguard
					has_trait = agot_winged_knight
				}
				NOR = {
					has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander
					has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
					has_council_position = battleguard_1
					has_council_position = winged_knight_1
					has_council_position = battleguard_2
					has_council_position = winged_knight_2
					has_council_position = battleguard_3
					has_council_position = winged_knight_3
					has_council_position = battleguard_4
					has_council_position = winged_knight_4
					has_council_position = battleguard_5
					has_council_position = winged_knight_5
					has_council_position = battleguard_6
					has_council_position = winged_knight_6
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = guard_to_repair
			scope:guard_owner = {
				agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = scope:guard_to_repair }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard }

			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
					has_trait = agot_rainbowguard
				}
				NOR = {
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = guard_to_repair
			scope:guard_owner = {
				agot_guard_assign_rainbow_guard_effect = { GUARD = scope:guard_to_repair }
			}
		}
		every_vassal_or_below = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
					has_trait = agot_rainbowguard
				}
				NOR = {
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
					has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6
				}

			save_scope_as = guard_to_repair
			scope:guard_owner = {

			}

	}
	if = {

			has_character_flag = has_kingsmen

					has_trait = agot_kingsmen

					has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander

				agot_guard_assign_kingsmen_effect = { GUARD = scope:guard_to_repair }
			}

				OR = {
					has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member
					has_trait = agot_kingsmen
				}
				NOR = {
					has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander
					has_council_position = kingsmen_1

					has_council_position = kingsmen_3
					has_council_position = kingsmen_4

agot_guard_positions_ai_fill_queensguard_slot_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_has_empty_queensguard_slot_trigger = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rhaenyra_106_queensguard }
			agot_guard_positions_fill_designated_rhaenyra_queensguard_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_courtier_or_guest = {
					agot_guard_can_be_queensguard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_council_position = queensguard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = queensguard_1
						has_council_position = queensguard_2
						has_council_position = queensguard_3
						has_council_position = queensguard_4
						has_council_position = queensguard_5
						has_council_position = queensguard_6
					}
				}
			}
			random_courtier_or_guest = {
				limit = {
					agot_guard_can_be_queensguard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_council_position = queensguard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = queensguard_1
						has_council_position = queensguard_2
						has_council_position = queensguard_3
						has_council_position = queensguard_4
						has_council_position = queensguard_5
						has_council_position = queensguard_6
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = ai_guard_candidate
				root = { agot_guard_positions_employ_queensguard_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_candidate } }
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_ai_fill_greenhand_slot_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_has_empty_greenhand_slot_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_fill_exhausted }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_courtier_or_guest = {
					agot_guard_can_be_greenhand_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					NOR = {
						has_council_position = greenhand_lord_commander
						has_council_position = greenhand_1
						has_council_position = greenhand_2
						has_council_position = greenhand_3
						has_council_position = greenhand_4
						has_council_position = greenhand_5
						has_council_position = greenhand_6
					}
				}
			}
			random_courtier_or_guest = {
				limit = {
					agot_guard_can_be_greenhand_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					NOR = {
						has_council_position = greenhand_lord_commander
						has_council_position = greenhand_1
						has_council_position = greenhand_2
						has_council_position = greenhand_3
						has_council_position = greenhand_4
						has_council_position = greenhand_5
						has_council_position = greenhand_6
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = ai_guard_candidate
				root = { agot_guard_assign_greenhand_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_candidate } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_vassal_or_below = {
					any_courtier_or_guest = {
						agot_guard_can_be_greenhand_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
						NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
			}
			random_vassal_or_below = {
				limit = {
					any_courtier_or_guest = {
						agot_guard_can_be_greenhand_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
						NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
				random_courtier_or_guest = {
					limit = {
						agot_guard_can_be_greenhand_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
						NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
					}
					save_scope_as = ai_guard_candidate
					root = { agot_guard_assign_greenhand_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_candidate } }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_vassal_or_below = {
					agot_guard_can_be_greenhand_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					NOR = {
						has_council_position = greenhand_lord_commander
						has_council_position = greenhand_1
						has_council_position = greenhand_2
						has_council_position = greenhand_3
						has_council_position = greenhand_4
						has_council_position = greenhand_5
						has_council_position = greenhand_6
					}
				}
			}
			random_vassal_or_below = {
				limit = {
					agot_guard_can_be_greenhand_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					NOR = {
						has_council_position = greenhand_lord_commander
						has_council_position = greenhand_1
						has_council_position = greenhand_2
						has_council_position = greenhand_3
						has_council_position = greenhand_4
						has_council_position = greenhand_5
						has_council_position = greenhand_6
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = ai_guard_candidate
				root = { agot_guard_assign_greenhand_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_candidate } }
			}
		}
		else = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_greenhand_fill_exhausted }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_ai_fill_battleguard_slot_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			agot_guard_has_empty_battleguard_slot_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_guard_slots_initializing }
			# Set below when a fill attempt this cycle exhausts all three candidate
			# tiers with nobody found; skips repeating a doomed search on the
			# remaining calls in this same maintenance cycle. Cleared per-cycle by
			# agot_guard_positions_ai_fill_guard_assignments_effect.
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_fill_exhausted }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				any_courtier_or_guest = {
					agot_guard_can_be_battleguard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_trait = kingsguard
						has_trait = agot_rainbowguard
						has_trait = agot_kingsmen

						has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander

						has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
						has_council_position = battleguard_1
						has_council_position = winged_knight_1
						has_council_position = battleguard_2
						has_council_position = winged_knight_2
						has_council_position = battleguard_3
						has_council_position = winged_knight_3
						has_council_position = battleguard_4
						has_council_position = winged_knight_4
						has_council_position = battleguard_5
						has_council_position = winged_knight_5
						has_council_position = battleguard_6

						has_council_position = winged_knight_6
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6

						has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander
						has_council_position = kingsmen_1
						has_council_position = kingsmen_2
						has_council_position = kingsmen_3
						has_council_position = kingsmen_4
						has_council_position = kingsmen_5
						has_council_position = kingsmen_6
					}
				}
			}

			random_courtier_or_guest = {
				limit = {
					agot_guard_can_be_battleguard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_trait = kingsguard
						has_trait = agot_rainbowguard
						has_trait = agot_kingsmen

						has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander

						has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
						has_council_position = battleguard_1
						has_council_position = winged_knight_1
						has_council_position = battleguard_2
						has_council_position = winged_knight_2
						has_council_position = battleguard_3
						has_council_position = winged_knight_3
						has_council_position = battleguard_4
						has_council_position = winged_knight_4
						has_council_position = battleguard_5
						has_council_position = winged_knight_5
						has_council_position = battleguard_6

						has_council_position = winged_knight_6
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6

						has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander
						has_council_position = kingsmen_1
						has_council_position = kingsmen_2
						has_council_position = kingsmen_3
						has_council_position = kingsmen_4
						has_council_position = kingsmen_5
						has_council_position = kingsmen_6
					}
				}

				save_scope_as = ai_guard_candidate

				root = {
					agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_candidate }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_vassal_or_below = {
					any_courtier_or_guest = {
						agot_guard_can_be_battleguard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
						prowess >= 15
						NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
			}
			random_vassal_or_below = {
				limit = {
					any_courtier_or_guest = {
						agot_guard_can_be_battleguard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
						prowess >= 15
						NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
				random_courtier_or_guest = {
					limit = {
						agot_guard_can_be_battleguard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
						prowess >= 15
						NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
					}
					save_scope_as = ai_guard_candidate
					root = {
						agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_candidate }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_vassal_or_below = {
					agot_guard_can_be_battleguard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_trait = kingsguard
						has_trait = agot_rainbowguard
						has_trait = agot_kingsmen

						has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander

						has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
						has_council_position = battleguard_1
						has_council_position = winged_knight_1
						has_council_position = battleguard_2
						has_council_position = winged_knight_2
						has_council_position = battleguard_3
						has_council_position = winged_knight_3
						has_council_position = battleguard_4
						has_council_position = winged_knight_4
						has_council_position = battleguard_5
						has_council_position = winged_knight_5
						has_council_position = battleguard_6

						has_council_position = winged_knight_6
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6

						has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander
						has_council_position = kingsmen_1
						has_council_position = kingsmen_2
						has_council_position = kingsmen_3
						has_council_position = kingsmen_4
						has_council_position = kingsmen_5
						has_council_position = kingsmen_6
					}
				}
			}

			random_vassal_or_below = {
				limit = {
					agot_guard_can_be_battleguard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_trait = kingsguard
						has_trait = agot_rainbowguard
						has_trait = agot_kingsmen

						has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander

						has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
						has_council_position = battleguard_1
						has_council_position = winged_knight_1
						has_council_position = battleguard_2
						has_council_position = winged_knight_2
						has_council_position = battleguard_3
						has_council_position = winged_knight_3
						has_council_position = battleguard_4
						has_council_position = winged_knight_4
						has_council_position = battleguard_5
						has_council_position = winged_knight_5
						has_council_position = battleguard_6

						has_council_position = winged_knight_6
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6

						has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander
						has_council_position = kingsmen_1
						has_council_position = kingsmen_2
						has_council_position = kingsmen_3
						has_council_position = kingsmen_4
						has_council_position = kingsmen_5
						has_council_position = kingsmen_6
					}
				}

				save_scope_as = ai_guard_candidate

				root = {
					agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_candidate }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_fill_exhausted
		}
	}
}
agot_guard_positions_ai_fill_rainbow_guard_slot_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_has_empty_rainbow_guard_slot_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_fill_exhausted }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_courtier_or_guest = {
					agot_guard_can_be_rainbow_guard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_trait = kingsguard
						has_trait = agot_battleguard
						has_trait = agot_winged_knight
						has_trait = agot_kingsmen
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6
					}
				}
			}
			random_courtier_or_guest = {
				limit = {
					agot_guard_can_be_rainbow_guard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_trait = kingsguard
						has_trait = agot_battleguard
						has_trait = agot_winged_knight
						has_trait = agot_kingsmen
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = ai_guard_candidate
				root = { agot_guard_assign_rainbow_guard_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_candidate } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_vassal_or_below = {
					any_courtier_or_guest = {
						agot_guard_can_be_rainbow_guard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
						prowess >= 15
						NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
			}
			random_vassal_or_below = {
				limit = {
					any_courtier_or_guest = {
						agot_guard_can_be_rainbow_guard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
						prowess >= 15
						NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
				random_courtier_or_guest = {
					limit = {
						agot_guard_can_be_rainbow_guard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
						prowess >= 15
						NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
					}
					save_scope_as = ai_guard_candidate
					root = { agot_guard_assign_rainbow_guard_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_candidate } }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_vassal_or_below = {
					agot_guard_can_be_rainbow_guard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_trait = kingsguard
						has_trait = agot_battleguard
						has_trait = agot_winged_knight
						has_trait = agot_kingsmen
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6
					}
				}
			}
			random_vassal_or_below = {
				limit = {
					agot_guard_can_be_rainbow_guard_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_trait = kingsguard
						has_trait = agot_battleguard
						has_trait = agot_winged_knight
						has_trait = agot_kingsmen
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3

agot_guard_assign_rainbow_guard_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_has_empty_rainbow_guard_slot_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_transfer_unlanded_guard_to_holder_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ }
		agot_guard_positions_knight_guard_member_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ }
		$GUARD$ = {
			remove_character_modifier = agot_former_rainbow_guard
			agot_guard_positions_set_rainbow_guard_origin_armor_effect = yes
			add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member
			add_trait = agot_rainbowguard
			add_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_guard_ambition
			remove_character_flag = kingsguard_ambition
			remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard
			agot_guard_positions_remove_duplicate_knight_trait_effect = yes
			agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes
			agot_guard_positions_record_appointment_effect = { HOLDER = root }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_lord_commander } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = rainbow_guard_lord_commander target = $GUARD$ }
			$GUARD$ = { agot_guard_positions_record_lord_commander_effect = { HOLDER = root } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_1 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = rainbow_guard_1 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_2 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = rainbow_guard_2 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_3 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = rainbow_guard_3 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_4 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = rainbow_guard_4 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_5 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = rainbow_guard_5 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:rainbow_guard_6 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = rainbow_guard_6 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_has_empty_battleguard_slot_trigger = yes
			$GUARD$ = { NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes } }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_transfer_unlanded_guard_to_holder_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ }
		$GUARD$ = {
			remove_character_modifier = agot_former_battleguard
			add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
			add_trait = agot_battleguard
			add_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_guard_ambition
			remove_character_flag = kingsguard_ambition
			remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard
			agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes
			agot_guard_positions_record_appointment_effect = { HOLDER = root }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:battleguard_lord_commander } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = battleguard_lord_commander target = $GUARD$ }
			$GUARD$ = { agot_guard_positions_record_lord_commander_effect = { HOLDER = root } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:battleguard_1 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = battleguard_1 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:battleguard_2 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = battleguard_2 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:battleguard_3 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = battleguard_3 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:battleguard_4 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = battleguard_4 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:battleguard_5 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = battleguard_5 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:battleguard_6 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = battleguard_6 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
	}
}

			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:kingsmen_lord_commander } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = kingsmen_lord_commander target = $GUARD$ }
			$GUARD$ = { agot_guard_positions_record_lord_commander_effect = { HOLDER = root } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:kingsmen_1 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = kingsmen_1 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:kingsmen_2 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = kingsmen_2 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:kingsmen_3 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = kingsmen_3 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:kingsmen_4 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = kingsmen_4 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:kingsmen_5 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = kingsmen_5 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:kingsmen_6 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = kingsmen_6 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_assign_canon_winged_knight_effect = {
	# Canon Tourney of the Winged Knights roster. Unavailable characters are skipped.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_character_flag = has_winged_knights
			character:Hardyng_5 = { is_alive = yes is_capable_adult = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes } }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_transfer_unlanded_guard_to_holder_effect = { GUARD = character:Hardyng_5 }
		agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = character:Hardyng_5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_character_flag = has_winged_knights
			character:Corbray_3 = { is_alive = yes is_capable_adult = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes } }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_transfer_unlanded_guard_to_holder_effect = { GUARD = character:Corbray_3 }
		agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = character:Corbray_3 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_character_flag = has_winged_knights
			character:Redfort_5 = { is_alive = yes is_capable_adult = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes } }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_transfer_unlanded_guard_to_holder_effect = { GUARD = character:Redfort_5 }
		agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = character:Redfort_5 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_character_flag = has_winged_knights
			character:Lipps_2 = { is_alive = yes is_capable_adult = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes } }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_transfer_unlanded_guard_to_holder_effect = { GUARD = character:Lipps_2 }
		agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = character:Lipps_2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_character_flag = has_winged_knights
			character:ShettGT_3 = { is_alive = yes is_capable_adult = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes } }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_transfer_unlanded_guard_to_holder_effect = { GUARD = character:ShettGT_3 }
		agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = character:ShettGT_3 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_character_flag = has_winged_knights
			character:Coldwater_3 = { is_alive = yes is_capable_adult = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes } }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_transfer_unlanded_guard_to_holder_effect = { GUARD = character:Coldwater_3 }
		agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = character:Coldwater_3 }
	}
	agot_guard_positions_enroll_canon_minor_guards_effect = yes
}

agot_guard_positions_end_winged_knight_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:winged_knight_lord_commander }
		cp:winged_knight_lord_commander = { add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard }
		fire_councillor = cp:winged_knight_lord_commander
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:winged_knight_1 }
		cp:winged_knight_1 = { add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard }
		fire_councillor = cp:winged_knight_1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:winged_knight_2 }
		cp:winged_knight_2 = { add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard }
		fire_councillor = cp:winged_knight_2
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:winged_knight_3 }
		cp:winged_knight_3 = { add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard }
		fire_councillor = cp:winged_knight_3
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:winged_knight_4 }
		cp:winged_knight_4 = { add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard }
		fire_councillor = cp:winged_knight_4
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:winged_knight_5 }
		cp:winged_knight_5 = { add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard }
		fire_councillor = cp:winged_knight_5
	}
	if = {
		limit = { exists = cp:winged_knight_6 }
		cp:winged_knight_6 = { add_character_flag = can_fire_kingsguard }
		fire_councillor = cp:winged_knight_6
	}
	every_courtier_or_guest = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
		remove_trait = agot_winged_knight
	}
	every_vassal_or_below = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
		remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
		remove_trait = agot_winged_knight
	}
	every_vassal_or_below = {
		every_courtier_or_guest = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes }
			agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
			remove_trait = agot_winged_knight
		}
	}
	title:e_the_vale = { set_variable = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_disbanded }
	remove_character_flag = has_winged_knights

	# Disbanding the order also ends every active academy enrolment for it.
	every_living_character = {
		limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights }
		agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_ai_fill_winged_knight_slot_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_has_empty_winged_knight_slot_trigger = yes
			NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_fill_exhausted }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				any_courtier_or_guest = {
					agot_guard_can_be_winged_knight_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_trait = kingsguard
						has_trait = agot_rainbowguard
						has_trait = agot_kingsmen
