						has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
						has_council_position = winged_knight_1
						has_council_position = winged_knight_2
						has_council_position = winged_knight_3
						has_council_position = winged_knight_4
						has_council_position = winged_knight_5
						has_council_position = winged_knight_6

						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6

						has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander
						has_council_position = kingsmen_1
						has_council_position = kingsmen_2
						has_council_position = kingsmen_3
						has_council_position = kingsmen_4
						has_council_position = kingsmen_5
						has_council_position = kingsmen_6
					}
				}
			}

			random_courtier_or_guest = {
				limit = {
					agot_guard_can_be_winged_knight_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_trait = kingsguard
						has_trait = agot_rainbowguard
						has_trait = agot_kingsmen

						has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
						has_council_position = winged_knight_1
						has_council_position = winged_knight_2
						has_council_position = winged_knight_3
						has_council_position = winged_knight_4
						has_council_position = winged_knight_5
						has_council_position = winged_knight_6

						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6

						has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander
						has_council_position = kingsmen_1
						has_council_position = kingsmen_2
						has_council_position = kingsmen_3
						has_council_position = kingsmen_4
						has_council_position = kingsmen_5
						has_council_position = kingsmen_6
					}
				}

				save_scope_as = ai_guard_candidate

				root = {
					agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_candidate }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_vassal_or_below = {
					any_courtier_or_guest = {
						agot_guard_can_be_winged_knight_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
						prowess >= 15
						NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
			}
			random_vassal_or_below = {
				limit = {
					any_courtier_or_guest = {
						agot_guard_can_be_winged_knight_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
						prowess >= 15
						NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
					}
				}
				random_courtier_or_guest = {
					limit = {
						agot_guard_can_be_winged_knight_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
						prowess >= 15
						NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_custom_guard_council_position_trigger = yes }
					}
					save_scope_as = ai_guard_candidate
					root = {
						agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_candidate }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_vassal_or_below = {
					agot_guard_can_be_winged_knight_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_trait = kingsguard
						has_trait = agot_rainbowguard
						has_trait = agot_kingsmen

						has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
						has_council_position = winged_knight_1
						has_council_position = winged_knight_2
						has_council_position = winged_knight_3
						has_council_position = winged_knight_4
						has_council_position = winged_knight_5
						has_council_position = winged_knight_6

						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6

						has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander
						has_council_position = kingsmen_1
						has_council_position = kingsmen_2
						has_council_position = kingsmen_3
						has_council_position = kingsmen_4
						has_council_position = kingsmen_5
						has_council_position = kingsmen_6
					}
				}
			}

			random_vassal_or_below = {
				limit = {
					agot_guard_can_be_winged_knight_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
					prowess >= 15
					NOR = {
						has_trait = kingsguard
						has_trait = agot_rainbowguard
						has_trait = agot_kingsmen

						has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
						has_council_position = winged_knight_1
						has_council_position = winged_knight_2
						has_council_position = winged_knight_3
						has_council_position = winged_knight_4
						has_council_position = winged_knight_5
						has_council_position = winged_knight_6

						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5
						has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6

						has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander
						has_council_position = kingsmen_1
						has_council_position = kingsmen_2
						has_council_position = kingsmen_3
						has_council_position = kingsmen_4
						has_council_position = kingsmen_5
						has_council_position = kingsmen_6
					}
				}

				save_scope_as = ai_guard_candidate

				root = {
					agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = scope:ai_guard_candidate }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_fill_exhausted
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_has_empty_winged_knight_slot_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_transfer_unlanded_guard_to_holder_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ }
		$GUARD$ = {
			remove_character_modifier = agot_former_winged_knight
			add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
			remove_trait = agot_battleguard
			add_trait = agot_winged_knight
			add_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_guard_ambition
			remove_character_flag = kingsguard_ambition
			remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard
			agot_guard_positions_destroy_guard_armor_artifacts_effect = yes
			agot_guard_positions_ensure_guard_artifacts_effect = yes
			agot_guard_positions_record_appointment_effect = { HOLDER = root }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:winged_knight_lord_commander } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = winged_knight_lord_commander target = $GUARD$ }
			$GUARD$ = { agot_guard_positions_record_lord_commander_effect = { HOLDER = root } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:winged_knight_1 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = winged_knight_1 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:winged_knight_2 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = winged_knight_2 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:winged_knight_3 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = winged_knight_3 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:winged_knight_4 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = winged_knight_4 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:winged_knight_5 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = winged_knight_5 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = cp:winged_knight_6 } }
			assign_councillor_type = { type = winged_knight_6 target = $GUARD$ }
		}
	}
}




agot_guard_positions_induct_founding_guard_effect = {
	if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_queensguard } agot_guard_positions_employ_queensguard_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_battleguard } agot_guard_assign_battleguard_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_winged_knights } agot_guard_assign_winged_knight_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_swords_of_braavos } agot_guard_assign_swords_of_braavos_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard } agot_guard_assign_rainbow_guard_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_kingsmen } agot_guard_assign_kingsmen_effect = { GUARD = $GUARD$ } }
}
# A trainee may belong to exactly one academy. Native AGOT can independently
# restore training_for_kingsguard from history, so normalize all order markers
# against the Royal Guards training owner whenever academy maintenance runs.
agot_guard_positions_enforce_single_training_order_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { $OWNER$ = { ruler_has_kingsguard_trigger = yes } }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } remove_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos } remove_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } remove_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand }
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_battleguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_winged_knights
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_swords_of_braavos
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_rainbow_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsmen
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_queensguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_greenhand
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } } add_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard }
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $OWNER$ = { has_character_flag = has_battleguard } }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } remove_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos } remove_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } remove_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand }
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_winged_knights
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_swords_of_braavos
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_rainbow_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsmen
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_queensguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_greenhand
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } } add_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard }
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_battleguard
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $OWNER$ = { has_character_flag = has_winged_knights } }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos } remove_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } remove_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand }
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_battleguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_swords_of_braavos
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_rainbow_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsmen
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_queensguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_greenhand
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } } add_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights }
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_winged_knights
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $OWNER$ = { has_character_flag = has_swords_of_braavos } }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } remove_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } remove_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand }
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_battleguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_winged_knights
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_rainbow_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsmen
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_queensguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_greenhand
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos } } add_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos }
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_swords_of_braavos
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $OWNER$ = { has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard } }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } remove_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } remove_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand }
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_battleguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_winged_knights
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsmen
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_queensguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_greenhand
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } } add_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard }
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_rainbow_guard
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $OWNER$ = { has_character_flag = has_kingsmen } }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } remove_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } remove_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand }
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_battleguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_winged_knights
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_rainbow_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_queensguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_greenhand
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } } add_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen }
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsmen
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $OWNER$ = { has_character_flag = has_queensguard } }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } remove_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } remove_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand }
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_battleguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_winged_knights
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_rainbow_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsmen
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_greenhand
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } } add_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard }
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_queensguard
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $OWNER$ = { agot_guard_positions_has_greenhand_trigger = yes } }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } remove_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen }
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard }
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_battleguard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_winged_knights
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_rainbow_guard
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsmen
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_queensguard
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } } add_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand }
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_greenhand
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = {
	# A school trainee's AGOT squire relation belongs to the school lifecycle.
	# End it on release or graduation so a newly sworn guard cannot remain a squire.
	random_relation = {
		type = agot_knight
		remove_relation_agot_squire = root
	}
	remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_academy_mentor
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_owner }
		var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner = { remove_list_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_trainee_list target = prev } }
	}
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } remove_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos } remove_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } remove_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } remove_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } remove_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand }
	remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_owner
	remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_merit
	remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_rank
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_passed_final_trials } }
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_kingsguard
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_battleguard
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_winged_knights
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_swords_of_braavos
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_rainbow_guard
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_kingsmen
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_queensguard
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_greenhand
	}
	remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_novice
	remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_squire
	remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_senior_cadet
	remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_graduate
	remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire
	# Graduation is a permanent qualification, not an active enrollment state.
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_novice } remove_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_novice }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_squire } remove_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_squire }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_proven } remove_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_proven }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_ready } remove_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_ready }
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_battleguard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_winged_knights
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_swords_of_braavos
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_rainbow_guard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsmen
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_queensguard
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_greenhand
}

# Academy vows take precedence over ordinary knighting and Thaneship. External
# ceremony/battlefield effects may still attempt to complete those paths, so
# return a non-graduate student to the appropriate squire state without ending
# their enrollment. Graduation is the sole point at which the completed status
# may be retained and converted into sworn service.
agot_guard_positions_remove_premature_student_knighthood_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_is_academy_student_trigger = yes
			NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_academy_graduate_trigger = yes }
			OR = { is_agot_knight_trigger = yes has_trait = agot_thane has_trait = knight }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_trait = agot_thane } remove_trait = agot_thane }
		if = { limit = { has_trait = knight } remove_trait = knight }
		if = {
			limit = { is_agot_knight_trigger = yes }
			if = { limit = { has_trait = squire } remove_trait = squire }
			add_trait = squire
		}
		agot_guard_positions_sync_academy_squire_path_effect = yes
	}
}

# Lightweight compatibility pass for ceremony mods. This deliberately affects
# enrolled non-graduates only; ordinary children retain the active mod set's
# normal age and chance rules for knighthood and Thaneship.
agot_guard_positions_enforce_academy_oaths_effect = {
	every_in_list = {
		variable = agot_guard_positions_trainee_list
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_is_academy_student_trigger = yes
			NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_academy_graduate_trigger = yes }
			OR = { is_agot_knight_trigger = yes has_trait = agot_thane has_trait = knight }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_remove_premature_student_knighthood_effect = yes
	}
}

# Give each trainee an individual result. Fixed awards to every attendee made
# every scoreboard read 0, 4, 9, 19 regardless of ability or performance.
agot_guard_positions_training_camp_individual_result_effect = {
	random_list = {
		10 = { }
		25 = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit add = 1 } change_variable = { name = imperial_examination_score add = 1 } }
		30 = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit add = 2 } change_variable = { name = imperial_examination_score add = 2 } }
		20 = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit add = 3 } change_variable = { name = imperial_examination_score add = 3 } }
		10 = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit add = 4 } change_variable = { name = imperial_examination_score add = 4 } }
		5 = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit add = 6 } change_variable = { name = imperial_examination_score add = 6 } }
	}
	agot_guard_positions_refresh_training_scoreboard_effect = yes
}

# The native examination widget sorts when its guest-subset model changes, not
# whenever a character variable changes. Rebuild this trainee's entries after
# each score award so the visible ranking follows the live score.
agot_guard_positions_refresh_training_scoreboard_effect = {
	save_scope_as = academy_score_changed_character
	scope:activity ?= {
		remove_from_guest_subset = { name = entrants target = scope:academy_score_changed_character }
		add_to_guest_subset = { name = entrants target = scope:academy_score_changed_character phase = guard_academy_phase_assembly }
		add_to_guest_subset = { name = entrants target = scope:academy_score_changed_character phase = guard_academy_phase_instruction }
		add_to_guest_subset = { name = entrants target = scope:academy_score_changed_character phase = guard_academy_phase_examination }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = {
	# Migrate the former four-icon rank implementation into one genuine CK3
	# trait path. The merit variable remains the activity score and is mirrored
	# exactly into the tracked trait's XP.
	# This effect is also called while opening the academy ledger, including for
	# trainees created by older saves. Establish both numeric variables here so
	# every caller is safe and comparisons never dereference an unset variable.
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_merit } }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit value = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_rank } }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_adult = yes has_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_ready }
		add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_passed_final_trials
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit value = 100 }
	}
	# This literal legacy-trait check must run before the old trait is removed.
	# Using the new graduate trigger here would miss old saves which do not yet
	# possess the tracked student trait.
	if = { limit = { has_trait = agot_guard_academy_graduate } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_passed_final_trials }
	remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_novice
	remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_squire
	remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_senior_cadet
	remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_graduate
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_novice } remove_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_novice }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_squire } remove_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_squire }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_proven } remove_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_proven }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_ready } remove_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_training_rank_ready }
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_passed_final_trials }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit value = 100 }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_merit var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit >= 60 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_rank var:agot_guard_positions_training_rank < 2 } trigger_event = agot_guard_positions.3503 }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_merit var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit >= 25 }
		if = { limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_rank var:agot_guard_positions_training_rank < 1 } trigger_event = agot_guard_positions.3503 }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 0 }
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_kingsguard
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_battleguard
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_winged_knights
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_swords_of_braavos
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_rainbow_guard
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_kingsmen
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_queensguard
		remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_greenhand
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_passed_final_trials }
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp value = 100 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_merit var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit >= 100 }
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp value = 99 }
		}
		else = {
			if = { 				limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_merit } 				set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp value = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit } 			} 			else = { set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp value = 0 } }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } add_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_kingsguard if = { limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } add_trait_xp = { trait = agot_guard_academy_student_kingsguard track = academy_kingsguard value = var:agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } add_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_battleguard if = { limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } add_trait_xp = { trait = agot_guard_academy_student_battleguard track = academy_battleguard value = var:agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } add_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_winged_knights if = { limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } add_trait_xp = { trait = agot_guard_academy_student_winged_knights track = academy_winged_knights value = var:agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos } add_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_swords_of_braavos if = { limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } add_trait_xp = { trait = agot_guard_academy_student_swords_of_braavos track = academy_swords_of_braavos value = var:agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } add_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_rainbow_guard if = { limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } add_trait_xp = { trait = agot_guard_academy_student_rainbow_guard track = academy_rainbow_guard value = var:agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } add_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_kingsmen if = { limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } add_trait_xp = { trait = agot_guard_academy_student_kingsmen track = academy_kingsmen value = var:agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } add_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_queensguard if = { limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } add_trait_xp = { trait = agot_guard_academy_student_queensguard track = academy_queensguard value = var:agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } add_trait = agot_guard_academy_student_greenhand if = { limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } add_trait_xp = { trait = agot_guard_academy_student_greenhand track = academy_greenhand value = var:agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp } } }
		remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_academy_path_xp
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_become_academy_instructor_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_instructor_owner value = var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner }
	if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_instructor_order_kingsguard }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_instructor_order_battleguard }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_instructor_order_winged_knights }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_instructor_order_swords_of_braavos }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_instructor_order_rainbow_guard }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_instructor_order_kingsmen }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_instructor_order_queensguard }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_instructor_order_greenhand }
	agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes
	add_trait = agot_guard_academy_instructor
}

agot_guard_positions_select_school_master_effect = {
	remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_school_master
	ordered_councillor = {
		position = 0
		order_by = { value = prowess add = martial }
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_active_guard_member_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root } }
		root = { set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_school_master value = prev } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_school_master }
			any_courtier_or_guest = { agot_guard_positions_is_instructor_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } }
		}
		ordered_courtier_or_guest = {
			position = 0
			order_by = { value = prowess add = martial }
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_instructor_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } }
			root = { set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_school_master value = prev } }
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_assign_trainee_to_serving_guard_effect = {
	$TRAINEE$ = { save_scope_as = guard_trainee }
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:guard_trainee = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_academy_student_trigger = yes
				has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_owner
				var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner ?= root
				agot_guard_positions_is_eligible_academy_squire_trigger = yes
				num_of_relation_agot_knight = 0
			}
			any_councillor = {
				agot_can_take_squire_trigger = yes
				OR = {
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } is_kingsguard_trigger = yes }
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes }
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes }
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes }
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } agot_guard_positions_is_kingsmen_trigger = yes }
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } agot_guard_positions_is_queensguard_trigger = yes }
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } agot_guard_positions_is_greenhand_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		ordered_councillor = {
			position = 0
			# Mentorship capacity is unlimited. Fill positions 1 through 7 once,
			# then repeat that same position order for every additional layer.
			order_by = {
				value = 0
				# Ordinary squires do not consume academy-distribution turns.
				every_relation = {
					type = agot_squire
					limit = {
						agot_guard_positions_is_academy_student_trigger = yes
						has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_owner
						var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner ?= root
					}
					subtract = 1000
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_council_position = kingsguard_1
							has_council_position = queensguard_lord_commander
							has_council_position = battleguard_lord_commander
							has_council_position = winged_knight_lord_commander
							has_council_position = rainbow_guard_lord_commander
							has_council_position = kingsmen_lord_commander
							has_council_position = greenhand_lord_commander
						}
					}
					add = 7
				}
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_council_position = kingsguard_2 has_council_position = queensguard_1 has_council_position = battleguard_1 has_council_position = winged_knight_1 has_council_position = rainbow_guard_1 has_council_position = kingsmen_1 has_council_position = greenhand_1 } } add = 6 }
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_council_position = kingsguard_3 has_council_position = queensguard_2 has_council_position = battleguard_2 has_council_position = winged_knight_2 has_council_position = rainbow_guard_2 has_council_position = kingsmen_2 has_council_position = greenhand_2 } } add = 5 }
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_council_position = kingsguard_4 has_council_position = queensguard_3 has_council_position = battleguard_3 has_council_position = winged_knight_3 has_council_position = rainbow_guard_3 has_council_position = kingsmen_3 has_council_position = greenhand_3 } } add = 4 }
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_council_position = kingsguard_5 has_council_position = queensguard_4 has_council_position = battleguard_4 has_council_position = winged_knight_4 has_council_position = rainbow_guard_4 has_council_position = kingsmen_4 has_council_position = greenhand_4 } } add = 3 }
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_council_position = kingsguard_6 has_council_position = queensguard_5 has_council_position = battleguard_5 has_council_position = winged_knight_5 has_council_position = rainbow_guard_5 has_council_position = kingsmen_5 has_council_position = greenhand_5 } } add = 2 }
				# Native AGOT exposes Kingsguard positions 1-6; its Lord Commander is
				# represented separately. Custom orders expose a seventh member slot.
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_council_position = queensguard_6 has_council_position = battleguard_6 has_council_position = winged_knight_6 has_council_position = rainbow_guard_6 has_council_position = kingsmen_6 has_council_position = greenhand_6 } } add = 1 }
			}
			limit = {
				agot_can_take_squire_trigger = yes
				OR = {
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } is_kingsguard_trigger = yes }
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard } agot_guard_positions_is_battleguard_trigger = yes }
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights } agot_guard_positions_is_winged_knight_trigger = yes }
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard } agot_guard_positions_is_rainbow_guard_trigger = yes }
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen } agot_guard_positions_is_kingsmen_trigger = yes }
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard } agot_guard_positions_is_queensguard_trigger = yes }
					AND = { scope:guard_trainee = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand } agot_guard_positions_is_greenhand_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = guard_training_mentor
		}
		agot_set_squire_effect = { KNIGHT = scope:guard_training_mentor SQUIRE = scope:guard_trainee }
		scope:guard_trainee = { agot_guard_positions_sync_academy_squire_path_effect = yes }
		# The relation is the authoritative one-to-many mentor record. Do not keep
		# a singular guard-side variable: one guard may mentor any number of squires.
		scope:guard_trainee = { set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_academy_mentor value = scope:guard_training_mentor } }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_sync_academy_squire_path_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			num_of_relation_agot_knight > 0
			agot_guard_positions_should_be_thane_trigger = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { has_trait = squire } remove_trait = squire }
		if = { limit = { has_trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire } remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire }
		add_trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_thane_squire_xp value = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit }
		if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_thane_squire_xp > 99 } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_thane_squire_xp value = 99 } }
		add_trait_xp = { trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire track = thane value = var:agot_guard_positions_thane_squire_xp }
		remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_thane_squire_xp
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { num_of_relation_agot_knight > 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire } remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_trait = squire } } add_trait = squire }
	}
	else = {
		if = { limit = { has_trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire } remove_trait = agot_guard_academy_thane_squire }
	}
}

# Shared, deliberately safe academy-contest progression. A minor wound is
# possible, while a fatal training accident is limited to one chance in 1,000.
agot_guard_positions_apply_mock_tournament_training_effect = {
	change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit add = $MERIT$ }
	if = {
		limit = { is_adult = yes }
		add_martial_lifestyle_xp = 35
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_trait = wounded_1 }
			NOT = { has_trait = wounded_2 }
			NOT = { has_trait = wounded_3 }
		}
		random = { chance = 8 add_trait = wounded_1 }
	}
	random_list = {
		999 = { }
		1 = { death = { death_reason = death_accident } }
	}
	agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = yes
}


# Tournament of the Guard Academies role override. Every character who is
# actively enrolled academy student is a contestant regardless of age.
# Everyone else who attends -- parents, sworn guards, instructors and guard
# holders included -- is a spectator. This is called after native tournament
# registration so CK3 cannot demote child trainees to spectators.
agot_guard_positions_force_national_academy_tournament_role_effect = {
	save_scope_as = academy_national_role_character
	if = {
		# A guard holder is never a contestant in a guard-academy tournament.
		# This wins over any stale or accidental Training to Join state.
		limit = {
			OR = {
				this = scope:host
				agot_guard_positions_has_active_guard_trigger = yes
				ruler_has_kingsguard_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		scope:activity = {
			remove_from_guest_subset = { name = contestant target = scope:academy_national_role_character }
			add_to_guest_subset = { name = spectator target = scope:academy_national_role_character }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_actively_enrolled_in_any_academy_trigger = yes }
		scope:activity = {
			remove_from_guest_subset = { name = spectator target = scope:academy_national_role_character }
			add_to_guest_subset = { name = contestant target = scope:academy_national_role_character }
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:activity = {
			remove_from_guest_subset = { name = contestant target = scope:academy_national_role_character }
			add_to_guest_subset = { name = spectator target = scope:academy_national_role_character }
		}
	}
}

# Native tournament phases recalculate participation at every contest and
# normally exclude children. Academy tournaments instead use enrollment alone:
# every trainee of this school competes, while the holder and supervisors only
# spectate. Calling this after each native registration keeps brackets, names,
# wagers and winner scopes pointed at the same contestant subset.
agot_guard_positions_force_mock_tournament_role_effect = {
	save_scope_as = academy_role_character
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { this = scope:host }
			NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_active_guard_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { ruler_has_kingsguard_trigger = yes }
			agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = scope:host }
		}
		scope:activity = {
			remove_from_guest_subset = { name = spectator target = scope:academy_role_character }
			add_to_guest_subset = { name = contestant target = scope:academy_role_character }
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:activity = {
			remove_from_guest_subset = { name = contestant target = scope:academy_role_character }
			add_to_guest_subset = { name = spectator target = scope:academy_role_character }
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_mock_phase_midpoint_effect = {
	scope:activity = {
		random_attending_character = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } }
			save_scope_as = featured_academy_candidate
		}
		every_attending_character = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } }
			change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit add = 2 }
			agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_mock_phase_final_effect = {
	scope:activity = {
		ordered_attending_character = {
			position = 0
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } }
			order_by = { value = prowess add = martial add = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit }
			save_scope_as = academy_phase_winner
		}
	}
	scope:academy_phase_winner ?= {
		change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit add = 8 }
		add_prestige = 75
		agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = yes
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_resolve_national_discipline_effect = {
	scope:activity = {
		ordered_attending_character = {
			position = 0
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_actively_enrolled_in_any_academy_trigger = yes }
			order_by = { value = $PRIMARY$ add = $SECONDARY$ add = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit }
			save_scope_as = academy_national_winner
		}
	}
	scope:academy_national_winner ?= {
		change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit add = $MERIT$ }
		add_prestige = 50
		agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = yes
		var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner = {
			save_scope_as = academy_national_winning_school
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_national_points } }
				set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_national_points value = 0 }
			}
			change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_national_points add = $POINTS$ }
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_resolve_academy_house_honours_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_lion_score value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_stag_score value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_falcon_score value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_tower_score value = 0 }
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_house_lion_champion
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_house_stag_champion
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_house_falcon_champion
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_house_tower_champion
	scope:activity = {
		every_attending_character = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } }
			if = { limit = { agot_guard_positions_academy_house_first_trigger = yes } root = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_lion_score add = scope:prev.var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit } } }
			else_if = { limit = { agot_guard_positions_academy_house_second_trigger = yes } root = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_stag_score add = scope:prev.var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit } } }
			else_if = { limit = { agot_guard_positions_academy_house_third_trigger = yes } root = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_falcon_score add = scope:prev.var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit } } }
			else_if = { limit = { agot_guard_positions_academy_house_fourth_trigger = yes } root = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_tower_score add = scope:prev.var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit } } }
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_house_lion_score >= var:agot_guard_house_stag_score var:agot_guard_house_lion_score >= var:agot_guard_house_falcon_score var:agot_guard_house_lion_score >= var:agot_guard_house_tower_score } add_character_flag = agot_guard_house_lion_champion }
	else_if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_house_stag_score >= var:agot_guard_house_falcon_score var:agot_guard_house_stag_score >= var:agot_guard_house_tower_score } add_character_flag = agot_guard_house_stag_champion }
	else_if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_house_falcon_score >= var:agot_guard_house_tower_score } add_character_flag = agot_guard_house_falcon_champion }
	else = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_house_tower_champion }
	scope:activity = {
		ordered_attending_character = {
			position = 0
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root }
				OR = {
					AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_house_lion_champion } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_first_trigger = yes }
					AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_house_stag_champion } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_second_trigger = yes }
					AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_house_falcon_champion } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_third_trigger = yes }
					AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_house_tower_champion } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_fourth_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
			order_by = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit
			save_scope_as = academy_house_honours_captain
		}
	}
	scope:academy_house_honours_captain ?= { add_prestige = 75 }
	trigger_event = agot_guard_school_activity.0005
}

agot_guard_positions_select_four_academy_house_representatives_effect = {
	scope:activity = {
		ordered_attending_character = { position = 0 limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_first_trigger = yes } order_by = { value = prowess add = martial add = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit } save_scope_as = academy_lion_representative }
		ordered_attending_character = { position = 0 limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_second_trigger = yes } order_by = { value = prowess add = martial add = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit } save_scope_as = academy_stag_representative }
		ordered_attending_character = { position = 0 limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_third_trigger = yes } order_by = { value = prowess add = martial add = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit } save_scope_as = academy_falcon_representative }
		ordered_attending_character = { position = 0 limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_fourth_trigger = yes } order_by = { value = prowess add = martial add = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit } save_scope_as = academy_tower_representative }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_resolve_four_academy_house_melee_effect = {
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_lion_score value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_stag_score value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_falcon_score value = 0 }
	set_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_tower_score value = 0 }
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_house_lion_champion
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_house_stag_champion
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_house_falcon_champion
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_house_tower_champion
	scope:activity = {
		every_attending_character = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } }
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_melee_contribution value = prowess }
			change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_melee_contribution add = martial }
			change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_melee_contribution add = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit }
			if = { limit = { agot_guard_positions_academy_house_first_trigger = yes } root = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_lion_score add = scope:prev.var:agot_guard_positions_melee_contribution } } }
			else_if = { limit = { agot_guard_positions_academy_house_second_trigger = yes } root = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_stag_score add = scope:prev.var:agot_guard_positions_melee_contribution } } }
			else_if = { limit = { agot_guard_positions_academy_house_third_trigger = yes } root = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_falcon_score add = scope:prev.var:agot_guard_positions_melee_contribution } } }
			else_if = { limit = { agot_guard_positions_academy_house_fourth_trigger = yes } root = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_house_tower_score add = scope:prev.var:agot_guard_positions_melee_contribution } } }
			remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_melee_contribution
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_house_lion_score >= var:agot_guard_house_stag_score var:agot_guard_house_lion_score >= var:agot_guard_house_falcon_score var:agot_guard_house_lion_score >= var:agot_guard_house_tower_score } add_character_flag = agot_guard_house_lion_champion }
	else_if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_house_stag_score >= var:agot_guard_house_falcon_score var:agot_guard_house_stag_score >= var:agot_guard_house_tower_score } add_character_flag = agot_guard_house_stag_champion }
	else_if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_house_falcon_score >= var:agot_guard_house_tower_score } add_character_flag = agot_guard_house_falcon_champion }
	else = { add_character_flag = agot_guard_house_tower_champion }
	scope:activity = {
		ordered_attending_character = {
			position = 0
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root }
				OR = {
					AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_house_lion_champion } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_first_trigger = yes }
					AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_house_stag_champion } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_second_trigger = yes }
					AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_house_falcon_champion } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_third_trigger = yes }
					AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_house_tower_champion } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_fourth_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
			order_by = { value = prowess add = martial add = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit }
			save_scope_as = academy_house_melee_winner
		}
		every_attending_character = {
			limit = {
				agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root }
				OR = {
					AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_house_lion_champion } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_first_trigger = yes }
					AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_house_stag_champion } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_second_trigger = yes }
					AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_house_falcon_champion } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_third_trigger = yes }
					AND = { root = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_house_tower_champion } agot_guard_positions_academy_house_fourth_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
			change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit add = 4 }
			agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = yes
		}
	}
	scope:academy_house_melee_winner ?= {
		change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit add = 8 }
		if = { limit = { prowess < 40 } add_prowess_skill = 1 }
		agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = yes
	}
}

# Team disciplines score every attending trainee into their own academy total.
# The winning portrait is the strongest cadet of the winning order, but the
# points and result belong to the whole order team.
agot_guard_positions_resolve_national_team_effect = {
	scope:activity = {
		every_attending_character = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_actively_enrolled_in_any_academy_trigger = yes }
			var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner = { set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_national_team_score value = 0 } }
		}
		every_attending_character = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_actively_enrolled_in_any_academy_trigger = yes }
			var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner = {
				add_to_list = academy_national_team_holders
			}
			var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_national_team_score add = scope:prev.$PRIMARY$ } }
			var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner = { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_national_team_score add = scope:prev.$SECONDARY$ } }
		}
	}
	ordered_in_list = {
		list = academy_national_team_holders
		position = 0
		order_by = var:agot_guard_positions_national_team_score
		save_scope_as = academy_national_winning_school
	}
	scope:activity = {
		ordered_attending_character = {
			position = 0
			limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_owner var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner ?= scope:academy_national_winning_school }
			order_by = { value = $PRIMARY$ add = $SECONDARY$ add = var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit }
			save_scope_as = academy_national_winner
		}
	}
	scope:academy_national_winning_school ?= {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_national_points } }
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_national_points value = 0 }
		}
		change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_national_points add = $POINTS$ }
		remove_variable = agot_guard_positions_national_team_score
	}
	scope:academy_national_winner ?= { change_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit add = $MERIT$ } add_prestige = 50 agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = yes }
}

agot_guard_positions_select_national_champion_effect = {
	every_ruler = {
		limit = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_national_points var:agot_guard_positions_national_points > 0 }
		add_to_list = academy_national_scored_holders
	}
	ordered_in_list = {
		list = academy_national_scored_holders
		position = 0
		order_by = var:agot_guard_positions_national_points
		save_scope_as = academy_national_champion_holder
	}
	scope:academy_national_champion_holder ?= {
		add_prestige = 250
		every_courtier_or_guest = {
			limit = { agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = scope:academy_national_champion_holder } }
			add_prestige = 50
		}
	}
}

# Adopt AGOT's native training_for_kingsguard history into the full academy
# model. AGOT applies this modifier to future white cloaks such as Richard Horpe
# even when they are already adults and live outside the royal court.
agot_guard_positions_enforce_rainbow_canon_training_precedence_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			character:Baratheon_4 ?= {
				is_alive = yes
				has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard
			}
		}
		# Parmen is Renly's serving Lord Commander, never a Kingsguard student.
		character:Crane_5 ?= {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard }
				agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes
			}
		}
		# Loras belongs to Renly's canon academy until his much later service in
		# the White Book. Preserve Rainbow training if it is already established;
		# otherwise clear the stale native state so Renly's canon seeder can enrol him.
		character:Tyrell_13 ?= {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard }
				agot_guard_positions_enforce_single_training_order_effect = { OWNER = character:Baratheon_4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard }
				agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_try_seed_white_book_canon_trainee_effect = {
	# Game-start on_actions have no root scope. Preserve the current ruler
	# explicitly before entering the trainee scope.
	save_scope_as = white_book_academy_owner
	if = {
		limit = {
			$TRAINEE$ ?= {
				is_alive = yes
				# Canon future guards may be represented by AGOT as academy-bound
				# from infancy. Ordinary applications still begin at age five.
				age >= 0
				current_date < $JOIN_DATE$
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_any_guard_member_trigger = yes }
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						OR = { this = character:Tyrell_13 this = character:Crane_5 }
						character:Baratheon_4 = { has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard }
					}
				}
				NOR = {
					has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard
					has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights
					has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard
					has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen
					has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard
					has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_owner }
					var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner ?= scope:white_book_academy_owner
				}
			}
		}
		$TRAINEE$ = {
			# Canon seeding records academy membership without moving the character
			# from their historical court or breaking an existing squire relation.
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard } } add_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard }
			add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_owner value = scope:white_book_academy_owner }
			scope:white_book_academy_owner = {
				remove_list_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_trainee_list target = prev }
				add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_guard_positions_trainee_list target = prev }
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_merit } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit value = 0 } }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_rank } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_white_book_rank_seeded } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							# Use the literal completed-knighthood trait here. AGOT's
							# broader trigger can classify a squire at 100 squire XP as
							# a knight before their dated history grants the trait.
							has_trait = knight
							has_trait = agot_thane
							current_date >= $GRADUATE_DATE$
						}
					}
					if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit < 100 } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit value = 100 } }
					set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 3 }
					add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_passed_final_trials
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { OR = { current_date >= $SENIOR_DATE$ age >= 14 } }
					if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit < 60 } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit value = 60 } }
					if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_training_rank < 2 } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 2 } }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { OR = { has_trait = squire age >= 10 } }
					if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_training_merit < 25 } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit value = 25 } }
					if = { limit = { var:agot_guard_positions_training_rank < 1 } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 1 } }
				}
				add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_white_book_rank_seeded
			}
			agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = yes
			agot_guard_positions_assign_regional_academy_house_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_adopt_native_kingsguard_trainees_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { ruler_has_kingsguard_trigger = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_enforce_rainbow_canon_training_precedence_effect = yes
		# History can restore training_for_kingsguard after custom-order startup.
		# A sworn guard is already past academy candidacy, regardless of which
		# order they joined. Clear that stale state before adopting true trainees.
		every_living_character = {
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard
				agot_guard_positions_is_any_guard_member_trigger = yes
			}
			agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes
		}
		every_living_character = {
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_any_guard_member_trigger = yes }
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						OR = { this = character:Tyrell_13 this = character:Crane_5 }
						character:Baratheon_4 = { has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard }
					}
				}
				NOR = {
					has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard
					has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights
					has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard
					has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen
					has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard
					has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand
				}
				# Never steal a Royal Guards student from another academy merely because
				# native AGOT restored its historical Kingsguard-training modifier.
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_owner }
					var:agot_guard_positions_training_owner ?= root
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_owner }
					NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_academy_student_trigger = yes }
					AND = { is_adult = yes NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_academy_graduate_trigger = yes } }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_owner value = root }
			root = { add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_guard_positions_trainee_list target = prev } }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_merit } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit value = 0 } }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_rank } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 0 } }
			add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard
			if = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes }
				add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_passed_final_trials
				set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit value = 100 }
				set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 3 }
			}
			agot_guard_positions_assign_regional_academy_house_effect = yes
			agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Assign the student to one of the four houses belonging to their own order.
# All house traits share one trait group, so adding the correct regional trait
# also replaces a legacy or incorrectly assigned house from an older save.
agot_guard_positions_assign_regional_academy_house_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard
			NOR = {
				has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsguard_dragon
				has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsguard_crown
				has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsguard_blackwater
				has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsguard_white_tower
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsguard_dragon }
			25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsguard_crown }
			25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsguard_blackwater }
			25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsguard_white_tower }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard NOR = { has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_battleguard_direwolf has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_battleguard_weirwood has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_battleguard_trident has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_battleguard_winter_rose } }
		random_list = { 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_battleguard_direwolf } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_battleguard_weirwood } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_battleguard_trident } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_battleguard_winter_rose } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights NOR = { has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_winged_knights_falcon has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_winged_knights_moon has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_winged_knights_giants_lance has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_winged_knights_bloody_gate } }
		random_list = { 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_winged_knights_falcon } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_winged_knights_moon } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_winged_knights_giants_lance } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_winged_knights_bloody_gate } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard NOR = { has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_rainbow_guard_rainbow has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_rainbow_guard_storm has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_rainbow_guard_stag has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_rainbow_guard_shipbreaker } }
		random_list = { 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_rainbow_guard_rainbow } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_rainbow_guard_storm } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_rainbow_guard_stag } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_rainbow_guard_shipbreaker } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen NOR = { has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsmen_dragonstone has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsmen_fury has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsmen_light has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsmen_painted_table } }
		random_list = { 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsmen_dragonstone } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsmen_fury } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsmen_light } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_kingsmen_painted_table } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard NOR = { has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_queensguard_black_dragon has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_queensguard_syrax has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_queensguard_dragonstone has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_queensguard_black_crown } }
		random_list = { 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_queensguard_black_dragon } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_queensguard_syrax } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_queensguard_dragonstone } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_queensguard_black_crown } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand NOR = { has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_greenhand_green_hand has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_greenhand_golden_rose has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_greenhand_oak has_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_greenhand_arbor } }
		random_list = { 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_greenhand_green_hand } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_greenhand_golden_rose } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_greenhand_oak } 25 = { add_trait = agot_guard_academy_house_greenhand_arbor } }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = {
	# Repair the former age-14 canon setup bug. Those characters could receive an
	# order trait even though the adult-only council position rejected them,
	# which then caused academy enrollment to exclude them as serving members.
	if = {
		limit = { $TRAINEE$ ?= { is_alive = yes is_adult = no } }
		if = {
			limit = { $TRAINEE$ = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member } }
			$TRAINEE$ = { remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_winged_knight_member remove_trait = agot_winged_knight remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { $TRAINEE$ = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member } }
			$TRAINEE$ = { remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_rainbow_guard_member remove_trait = agot_rainbowguard remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { $TRAINEE$ = { has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member } }
			$TRAINEE$ = { remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_kingsmen_member remove_trait = agot_kingsmen remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			$TRAINEE$ ?= {
				is_alive = yes
				age >= 5
				age <= 15
				NOT = { is_agot_knight_trigger = yes }
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_any_guard_member_trigger = yes }
				# Opening the academy reruns canon-history repair. Never send an
				# existing student through fresh enrollment and erase their merit.
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } }
			}
		}
		# Historical Thaneship setup must not block a canon child from entering
		# the academy; enrollment owns progression until graduation.
		$TRAINEE$ = {
			if = { limit = { has_trait = agot_thane } remove_trait = agot_thane }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_enroll_guard_trainee_effect = { TRAINEE = $TRAINEE$ }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $TRAINEE$ ?= { is_alive = yes age < 5 NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_any_guard_member_trigger = yes } } }
		agot_guard_positions_reserve_canon_future_trainee_effect = { TRAINEE = $TRAINEE$ }
	}
}

# Robb's canon battle companions are reserved for the Battleguard school from
# squire age. This is deliberately separate from the generic minor helper:
# mature canon companions who are not among the designated seven still remain
# academy candidates instead of being discarded by the normal age ceiling.
agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = {
	# Apply the same repair to canon Battleguard children created by the former
	# age-14 setup boundary before testing whether they can enter training.
	if = {
		limit = { $TRAINEE$ ?= { is_alive = yes is_adult = no has_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member } }
		$TRAINEE$ = {
			remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_battleguard_member
			remove_trait = agot_battleguard
			remove_character_flag = blocked_from_leaving
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			$TRAINEE$ ?= {
				is_alive = yes
				age >= 5
				age <= 15
				NOT = { is_agot_knight_trigger = yes }
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_any_guard_member_trigger = yes }
				# Preserve merit/rank when the fixed-history repair sees a student
				# who is already enrolled in this Battleguard academy.
				NOT = { agot_guard_positions_is_training_for_active_order_trigger = { OWNER = root } }
			}
		}
		# A fixed-history Battleguard candidate takes precedence over a generic or
		# stale academy reservation. The shared enrollment effect below clears the
		# previous ambition before applying ROOT's Battleguard training state.
		# Canon academy enrollment takes precedence over historical Thaneship from
		# another mod. Do not impose an age here: the active AGOT/age-mod rules own
		# age eligibility, while this academy owns progression until graduation.
		$TRAINEE$ = {
			if = { limit = { has_trait = agot_thane } remove_trait = agot_thane }
		}
		agot_guard_positions_enroll_guard_trainee_effect = { TRAINEE = $TRAINEE$ }
	}
	else_if = {
