		limit = { $TRAINEE$ ?= { is_alive = yes age < 5 NOT = { agot_guard_positions_has_any_training_ambition_trigger = yes } } }
		agot_guard_positions_reserve_canon_future_trainee_effect = { TRAINEE = $TRAINEE$ }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_reserve_canon_future_trainee_effect = {
	# A canon reservation is full academy membership, not merely a modifier.
	# Keep the child at their historical court, but register them with the
	# holder's roster exactly as the White Book seeding path does.
	save_scope_as = guard_training_owner
	# Explicit custom-order canon history takes precedence over AGOT's generic
	# future-Kingsguard marker. Remove every previous academy identity before
	# applying the order selected by this canon seeder.
	$TRAINEE$ = { hidden_effect = { agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes } }
	if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_battleguard } $TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_battleguard } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_winged_knights } $TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_winged_knights } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard } $TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_rainbow_guard } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_kingsmen } $TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsmen } }
	else_if = { limit = { has_character_flag = has_queensguard } $TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_queensguard } }
	else_if = { limit = { agot_guard_positions_has_greenhand_trigger = yes } $TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_greenhand } }
	else_if = { limit = { ruler_has_kingsguard_trigger = yes } $TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard } }
	$TRAINEE$ = {
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_owner value = scope:guard_training_owner }
		scope:guard_training_owner = {
			remove_list_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_trainee_list target = prev }
			add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_guard_positions_trainee_list target = prev }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_merit } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = agot_guard_positions_training_rank } } set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 0 } }
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_passed_final_trials
		agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_assign_regional_academy_house_effect = yes
	}
}

# Fixed-history members who have not yet reached the guard appointment age are
# reserved for their canon order immediately. The yearly maintenance call also
# restores the intended state if history/setup ordering delayed enrolment.
agot_guard_positions_enroll_canon_minor_guards_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { this = character:Stark_3 this = character:Stark_6 }
			has_character_flag = has_battleguard
		}
		# Canon candidates who are alive and of academy age receive the same
		# reservation in 282 as in later starts. The helper's age and existing
		# service checks prevent them from entering Eddard's active six-member roster.
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Manderly_8 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Locke_2 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Norrey_4 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Flint_6 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Mallister_7 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Hornwood_3 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Tully_9 }

		# Remaining individually named canon members of Robb Stark's battle guard.
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Blackwood_11 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Frey_78 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Frey_81 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Greyjoy_14 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Karstark_17 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Karstark_18 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Mormont_5 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Umber_11 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_battleguard_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Westerling_4 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = character:Baratheon_4 has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard }
		# Parmen is already appointed in 284 AC. These six canon companions enter
		# the Rainbow Guard school from squire age and retain their reserved order.
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Tyrell_13 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Royce_5 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Caron_5 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Cuy_3 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Morrigen_4 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Tarth_6 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = character:Baratheon_3 has_character_flag = has_kingsmen }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Seaworth_1 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Estermont_7 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Gower_5 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Caron_6 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Baratheon_hh_2 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Tally_Hill_1 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Blackberry_12 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_winged_knights }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Hardyng_5 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Corbray_3 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Redfort_5 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Lipps_2 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:ShettGT_3 }
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Coldwater_3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = character:Targaryen_63 has_character_flag = has_queensguard }
		# Canon future Kingsguard and Lord Commander; at Rhaenyra's court he
		# begins as a Queensguard academy candidate.
		agot_guard_positions_try_enroll_canon_minor_effect = { TRAINEE = character:Fell_27 }
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_enroll_guard_trainee_effect = {
	# The current scope is the academy holder. Do not use root as the owner:
	# canon setup can call this effect from inside a holder scope while root still
	# belongs to the surrounding game-start on_action.
	save_scope_as = guard_training_owner
	if = {
		limit = {
			$TRAINEE$ = {
				NOT = { is_agot_knight_trigger = yes }
				NOT = { has_trait = agot_thane }
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = has_kingsmen }
				$TRAINEE$ = { is_male = yes }
			}
		}
	$TRAINEE$ = {
		hidden_effect = { agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_owner value = scope:guard_training_owner }
		# Keep the maintained index idempotent. Re-enrollment and old-save repair
		# must never leave duplicate entries which would duplicate academy cards.
		scope:guard_training_owner = {
			remove_list_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_trainee_list target = prev }
			add_to_variable_list = { name = agot_guard_positions_trainee_list target = prev }
		}
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_merit value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_training_rank value = 0 }
		remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_passed_final_trials
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_battleguard }
		$TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_battleguard add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_battleguard }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_winged_knights }
		$TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_winged_knights add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_winged_knights }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_swords_of_braavos }
		$TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_swords_of_braavos add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_swords_of_braavos }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_rainbow_guard }
		$TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_rainbow_guard add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_rainbow_guard }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_kingsmen }
		$TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_kingsmen add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsmen }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = has_queensguard }
		$TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_queensguard add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_queensguard }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { agot_guard_positions_has_greenhand_trigger = yes }
		$TRAINEE$ = { add_character_modifier = training_for_greenhand add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_greenhand }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { ruler_has_kingsguard_trigger = yes }
		$TRAINEE$ = {
			add_character_modifier = training_for_kingsguard
			add_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_training_order_kingsguard
		}
	}
	$TRAINEE$ = {
		agot_guard_positions_enforce_single_training_order_effect = { OWNER = scope:guard_training_owner }
		agot_guard_positions_update_training_rank_effect = yes
		agot_guard_positions_assign_regional_academy_house_effect = yes
		if = { limit = { is_adult = no NOT = { has_focus = education_martial } } set_focus = education_martial }
	}
	# Accepted, unlanded students study at the academy holder's court. Do this
	# explicitly from the saved current holder scope: enrollment is also called
	# from nested game-start scopes where ROOT is not necessarily that holder.
	$TRAINEE$ ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_landed = no
				NOT = { is_courtier_of = scope:guard_training_owner }
			}
			scope:guard_training_owner = { add_courtier = prev }
		}
	}
	agot_guard_positions_assign_trainee_to_serving_guard_effect = { TRAINEE = $TRAINEE$ }
	}
}

# Build the eligible-academy set globally, then select the geographically
# closest qualifying holder. Admission suitability is established first by the
# full order-specific training trigger; distance only ranks valid schools.
agot_guard_positions_send_to_local_academy_effect = {
	save_scope_as = guard_school_sender
	$TRAINEE$ = { save_scope_as = guard_school_trainee }
	every_ruler = {
		limit = {
			is_alive = yes
			OR = {
				agot_guard_positions_has_active_guard_trigger = yes
				ruler_has_kingsguard_trigger = yes
			}
			scope:guard_school_trainee = {
				agot_guard_positions_can_train_for_owner_order_trigger = { OWNER = prev }
			}
		}
		add_to_list = eligible_local_guard_academies
	}
	ordered_in_list = {
		list = eligible_local_guard_academies
		position = 0
		order_by = {
			value = 0
			subtract = "capital_county.squared_distance(scope:guard_school_sender.capital_province)"
		}
		save_scope_as = selected_local_guard_academy
	}
	scope:selected_local_guard_academy ?= {
		# Admission is an application, not an automatic transfer. The academy
		# holder decides before the trainee changes court or gains any modifiers.
		trigger_event = agot_guard_school_admission.0001
	}
}
agot_guard_positions_appoint_training_candidate_effect = {
	$CANDIDATE$ = { save_scope_as = kingsguard_candidate }
	if = {
		limit = { ruler_primary_title_has_kingsguard_trigger = yes }
		$CANDIDATE$ = { trigger_event = agot_kingsguard.1004 }
	}
	else = {
		add_courtier ?= $CANDIDATE$
		$CANDIDATE$ = { agot_guard_positions_clear_training_ambition_effect = yes }
		agot_guard_positions_assign_active_guard_effect = { GUARD = $CANDIDATE$ }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1002 days = 1 }
		trigger_event = { id = agot_guard_positions.1004 days = 1 }
	}
}
# Called when one guard returns. The six-month recovery applies only to that
# character, leaving every other member free to be dispatched immediately.
agot_guard_positions_finish_mission_effect = {
	remove_character_flag = agot_guard_positions_on_mission
	remove_character_modifier = agot_guard_positions_away_on_mission
	hidden_effect = { set_location_to_default = yes }
	add_character_modifier = {
		modifier = agot_guard_positions_mission_cooldown
		months = 6
	}
}

agot_guard_positions_assign_ward_escort_effect = {
	$GUARD$ = {
		add_character_flag = { flag = agot_guard_positions_on_mission years = 20 }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = agot_guard_positions_ward_escort }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_escorted_ward value = $WARD$ }
		if = { limit = { exists = scope:ward_guardian_destination } set_location = scope:ward_guardian_destination }
		else = { set_location = $WARD$.location }
	}
	random_councillor = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_guard_available_for_mission_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
			NOT = { this = $GUARD$ }
		}
		add_character_flag = { flag = agot_guard_positions_on_mission years = 2 }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = agot_guard_positions_away_on_mission }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_temporary_escort_ward value = $WARD$ }
		if = { limit = { exists = scope:ward_guardian_destination } set_location = scope:ward_guardian_destination }
		else = { set_location = $WARD$.location }
		save_scope_as = temporary_ward_escort_2
	}
	random_councillor = {
		limit = {
			agot_guard_positions_guard_available_for_mission_trigger = { COURT_OWNER = root }
			NOT = { this = $GUARD$ }
			NOT = { this = scope:temporary_ward_escort_2 }
		}
		add_character_flag = { flag = agot_guard_positions_on_mission years = 2 }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = agot_guard_positions_away_on_mission }
		set_variable = { name = agot_guard_positions_temporary_escort_ward value = $WARD$ }
		if = { limit = { exists = scope:ward_guardian_destination } set_location = scope:ward_guardian_destination }
		else = { set_location = $WARD$.location }
	}
}
